# TPA Pro — Structure

```
TPA/
├── index.html              # Entry: links CSS + loads JS in dependency order
├── STRUCTURE.md
├── css/
│   ├── fonts.css           # Tajawal (local woff2)
│   ├── variables.css       # :root CSS variables + base reset
│   ├── screens.css         # splash / loading / lock
│   ├── layout.css          # shell / sidebar / topbar
│   ├── components.css      # buttons / inputs / cards / KPI
│   ├── tables-1.css, tables-2.css
│   └── modals-1..3.css
├── js/
│   ├── shell-*.js          # HTML shell fragments (splash/loading/lock/app/modals)
│   ├── shell-loader.js     # Injects shell into #root
│   ├── html-page-*.js      # Per-page HTML templates (PAGE_*)
│   ├── html-modal-*.js     # Per-modal HTML templates (MODAL_*)
│   ├── utils*.js           # General helpers, dates, numbers, Arabic, DOM
│   ├── constants.js        # App constants & settings
│   ├── data-*.js           # Models, seed data, claims
│   ├── entities.js         # Entity helpers
│   ├── storage.js          # Persistence layer
│   ├── engine-*.js         # Field extraction, rules engine
│   ├── compute-patient.js  # Core computePatient
│   ├── dialog.js           # Unified dialog
│   ├── navigation.js       # Screen navigation
│   ├── pages-build.js      # buildPages()
│   ├── modals-build.js     # buildModals()
│   ├── export.js           # Excel/PDF/Print export
│   ├── render*.js          # Rendering functions
│   ├── review-*.js         # Review modal logic
│   ├── editors.js, item-editor.js
│   ├── claims-view*.js
│   ├── intake*.js
│   ├── providers*.js       # providers/contracts/tariffs
│   ├── trash.js, deduction-rules.js, rule-wizard*.js
│   ├── sessions*.js, backup*.js
│   ├── auth-settings.js, notifications.js
│   ├── app.js              # initApp, renderAll
│   └── init.js             # Loading + bootstrap
└── assets/fonts/           # Tajawal woff2 (10 files)
```

All files ≤ 150 lines. No external dependencies (fully offline).
Load order is defined in index.html (82 script tags).

FILE: /home/administrator/TPA/sw.js
================================================================================
const CACHE_NAME = 'tpa-pro-v1.0.3';

self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => cache.addAll(['./', './index.html', './manifest.json'])).then(() => self.skipWaiting())
  );
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then((keys) =>
      Promise.all(keys.filter((k) => k !== CACHE_NAME).map((k) => caches.delete(k)))
    ).then(() => self.clients.claim())
  );
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', (event) => {
  const req = event.request;
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return;
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (url.origin !== self.location.origin) return;
  event.respondWith(
    caches.match(req).then((cached) => {
      if (cached) return cached;
      return fetch(req).then((res) => {
        if (res && res.status === 200 && res.type === 'basic') {
          const clone = res.clone();
          caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((c) => c.put(req, clone));
        }
        return res;
      }).catch(() => {
        if (req.mode === 'navigate') {
          return caches.match('./index.html');
        }
        return new Response('', { status: 503, statusText: 'Offline' });
      });
    })
  );
});

function renderAll(){
  renderKPIs(); renderRecent(); renderDonut(); renderReasonBars('#reasonBars');
  renderClaims(); renderIntake(); renderProviders(); renderContracts();
  renderTariffs(); renderReports(); renderSearch(); renderTrash(); renderDeductionRules();
  renderSessions();
  updateTrashBadge(); updateBackupStats();
  persistData();
}
let appReady = false;
function initApp(){
  if(!appReady){
    appReady = true;
    bindNav();
    if(typeof bindMobile === 'function') bindMobile();
    if(typeof bindShortcuts === 'function') bindShortcuts();
    const langBtn = document.getElementById('tbLangBtn');
    if(langBtn){
      langBtn.textContent = currentLang === 'ar' ? 'EN' : 'AR';
      langBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { setLang(currentLang === 'ar' ? 'en' : 'ar'); langBtn.textContent = currentLang === 'ar' ? 'EN' : 'AR'; });
    }
    if(typeof applyAllTranslations === 'function') applyAllTranslations();
    bindClaimsControls();
    bindReviewModal();
    bindProvidersControls();
    bindContractsControls();
    bindTariffsControls();
    bindIntakeControls();
    bindSearchControls();
    bindTrashControls();
    bindDeductionRulesControls();
    bindSessionsControls();
    $('#timelineBackBtn').addEventListener('click', goBack);
    $('#timelineReviewBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { if(currentTimelineClaimId) openReview(currentTimelineClaimId); });
    let t1, t2;
    $('#dashSearch').addEventListener('input', e => { clearTimeout(t1); t1 = setTimeout(() => { dashSearchTerm = e.target.value.trim(); renderRecent(); }, 200); });
    $('#reportsSearch').addEventListener('input', e => { clearTimeout(t2); t2 = setTimeout(() => { reportsSearchTerm = e.target.value.trim(); renderReports(); }, 200); });
    currentPage = 'dashboard';
    if(typeof setLang === 'function') setLang(currentLang || 'ar');
    if(typeof applyPage === 'function') applyPage('dashboard');
    $('#logoutBtn').addEventListener('click', logout);
    $('#logoutBtn2').addEventListener('click', logout);

    // أزرار التصدير/الاستيراد
    const _eB = $('#exportBackupBtn'), _eS = $('#exportSettingsBtn'), _eSess = $('#exportSessionsBtn'), _iB = $('#importBackupBtn'), _iF = $('#importBackupFile');
    if(_eB) _eB.addEventListener('click', exportBackup);
    if(_eS) _eS.addEventListener('click', exportSettings);
    if(_eSess) _eSess.addEventListener('click', exportSessions);
    if(_iB && _iF){ _iB.addEventListener('click', () => _iF.click()); _iF.addEventListener('change', e => { const f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0]; if(f) importBackupFile(f); e.target.value = ''; }); }

    // تخصيص
    const saveSet = $('#saveAppSettingsBtn'), resetSet = $('#resetAppSettingsBtn');
    if(saveSet) saveSet.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const n = ($('#setReviewerName')?.value||'').trim(), r = ($('#setReviewerRole')?.value||'').trim();
      const cn = ($('#setCompanyName')?.value||'').trim(), cb = ($('#setCompanyBrand')?.value||'').trim(), ct = ($('#setCompanyTagline')?.value||'').trim();
      if(!n){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل اسم المراجع'); return; }
      if(!cb){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل العلامة'); return; }
      appSettings.reviewer.name = n; appSettings.reviewer.role = r || 'مراجع مالي معتمد';
      appSettings.company.name = cn || 'TPA'; appSettings.company.brand = cb; appSettings.company.tagline = ct || appSettings.company.tagline;
      saveAppSettings(); applyAppSettingsToUI(); updateBackupStats(); showSuccess('تم','تم');
    });
    if(resetSet) resetSet.addEventListener('click', () => {
      openDialog({ type:'warning', title:'استعادة', message:'سيتم إعادة التعيين.', confirmText:'استعادة', cancelText:'إلغاء',
        onConfirm: () => { appSettings = { reviewer:{ name:'المراجع', role:'مراجع مالي معتمد' }, company:{ name:'TPA', brand:'TPA Pro', tagline:'نظام المراجعة المالية', version:'v1.0' } }; saveAppSettings(); applyAppSettingsToUI(); updateBackupStats(); showSuccess('تم','تم'); } });
    });

    // كلمة المرور
    const chgBtn = $('#changePwdBtn');
    if(chgBtn) chgBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const oldP = $('#oldPwd').value, newP = $('#newPwd').value, confP = $('#confirmPwd').value;
      if(!oldP || !newP){ showWarning('تنبيه','املأ الحقول'); return; }
      if(newP !== confP){ showWarning('تنبيه','التأكيد لا يطابق'); return; }
      try { await changePassword(oldP, newP); $('#oldPwd').value = ''; $('#newPwd').value = ''; $('#confirmPwd').value = ''; showSuccess('تم','تم التغيير'); }
      catch(err){ showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
    });

    // مسح البيانات
    const resetAll = $('#resetAllDataBtn');
    if(resetAll) resetAll.addEventListener('click', () => {
      showDelete('مسح كل البيانات', 'سيتم حذف كل شيء والعودة للافتراضي. <b>لا يمكن التراجع.</b>', () => {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(DATA_STORE_KEY); localStorage.removeItem(SESSIONS_KEY); localStorage.removeItem(LAST_BACKUP_KEY); localStorage.removeItem(PWD_HASH_KEY); showSuccess('تم','إعادة التحميل...'); setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 1200); }
        catch(e){ showWarning('خطأ', e.message); }
      });
    });

    // شريط علوي
    const tbB = document.getElementById('tbBackupBtn'), tbU = document.getElementById('tbUserBtn'), tbT = document.getElementById('tbTrashBtn');
    if(tbB) tbB.addEventListener('click', () => doExportBackup(false));
    if(tbT) tbT.addEventListener('click', () => { if(currentPage && currentPage !== 'trash') navHistory.push({ page: currentPage, viewType: viewState.type, viewId: viewState.id }); navigateTo('trash', {noPush:true}); });
    const sb = document.getElementById('smartBackBtn');
    if(sb) sb.addEventListener('click', goBack);
    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => { const m = document.getElementById('tbUserMenu'); if(m && !e.target.closest('#tbUserWrap')) m.style.display = 'none'; });
    const tm = document.getElementById('tbUserMenu');
    if(tm) tm.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const btn = e.target.closest('.tb-menu-item'); if(!btn) return;
      const action = btn.dataset.action;
      const m = document.getElementById('tbUserMenu'); if(m) m.style.display = 'none';
    if(action === 'help'){ navHistory = []; navigateTo('help', {noPush:true}); }
    if(action === 'settings'){ navHistory = []; navigateTo('settings', {noPush:true}); }
    if(action === 'lang'){ setLang(currentLang === 'ar' ? 'en' : 'ar'); const item = tm.querySelector('[data-action=\"lang\"]'); if(item) item.textContent = currentLang === 'ar' ? 'اللغة: EN' : 'Language: AR'; }
    });

    // ⏱️ حفظ تلقائي كل 20 ثانية
    if(!window._autosaveInterval){
      window._autosaveInterval = setInterval(() => {
        try { if(activeClaim && document.getElementById('reviewModal')?.classList.contains('open')) upsertCurrentSession(); } catch(e){}
      }, 20000);
    }

    // ⚠️ تحذير قبل الإغلاق
    if(!window._unloadBound){
      window._unloadBound = true;
      window.addEventListener('beforeunload', (e) => {
        try {
          if(activeClaim && document.getElementById('reviewModal')?.classList.contains('open')){
            upsertCurrentSession();
            if(_hasUnsavedChanges()){ e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ''; return ''; }
          }
        } catch(err){}
      });
    }

    // ⌨️ اختصارات لوحة المفاتيح
    document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
      if(document.getElementById('reviewModal')?.classList.contains('open')){
        if((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 's'){ e.preventDefault(); saveReview(); return; }
        if((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'Enter'){ e.preventDefault(); finalizeReview(); return; }
      }
      if((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === '/'){
        e.preventDefault();
        if(currentPage === 'claims') $('#searchInput')?.focus();
        else if(currentPage === 'search') $('#srchQuery')?.focus();
        else if(currentPage === 'sessions') $('#sessionsSearch')?.focus();
      }
    });

    loadSessions(); updateSessionsBadge();
  }
  renderAll();
  setTimeout(() => { if(typeof translateDOMMulti === 'function') translateDOMMulti(); else if(typeof translateDOM === 'function') translateDOM(document.body); }, 0);
}

function logoutWithReminder(){
  if(hasRecentBackup(3600000) && !(typeof activeClaim !== 'undefined' && activeClaim)){
    openDialog({ type:'info', title:'تسجيل الخروج', message:`آخر نسخة ${getLastBackupAgo()}. خروج؟`, confirmText:'خروج', cancelText:'إلغاء',
      onConfirm: () => { localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); go('lock'); setTimeout(()=>$('#pwd').focus(),300); } });
    return;
  }
  openDialog({
    type:'warning', title:'نسخة احتياطية', message:'لم تُنشئ نسخة حديثة.',
    body: `<div style="background:#F8FAFC;border:1px solid #E2E8F0;border-radius:10px;padding:12px 16px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.9;text-align:center"><div><b style="color:var(--navy);font-size:15px">${claims.length}</b> مطالبة · <b style="color:var(--navy);font-size:15px">${providers.length}</b> جهة · <b style="color:var(--navy);font-size:15px">${workSessions.length}</b> جلسة</div><div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px;margin-top:4px">آخر: ${getLastBackupAgo() || 'لم'}</div></div><div style="margin-top:14px;text-align:center"><button type="button" class="btn-xs danger" onclick="confirmLogoutWithoutBackup()" style="padding:9px 18px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700">خروج بدون نسخة</button></div>`,
    confirmText:'تصدير وخروج', cancelText:'إلغاء',
    onConfirm: () => { doExportBackup(true); setTimeout(() => { localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); go('lock'); setTimeout(()=>$('#pwd').focus(),300); }, 500); }
  });
}
function confirmLogoutWithoutBackup(){
  closeDialog();
  setTimeout(() => {
    openDialog({ type:'danger', title:'خروج', message:'متأكد من الخروج بدون نسخة؟', confirmText:'خروج', cancelText:'إلغاء',
      onConfirm: () => { localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); go('lock'); setTimeout(()=>$('#pwd').focus(),300); } });
  }, 200);
}
function bindSettingsPassword(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#page-settings .onb-eye').forEach(btn => {
    if(btn.dataset.bound) return; btn.dataset.bound='1';
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const inp = document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target);
      if(!inp) return;
      const show = inp.type === 'password';
      inp.type = show ? 'text' : 'password';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label', show ? 'إخفاء' : 'إظهار');
    });
  });
  const newPwd = document.getElementById('newPwd');
  const bar = document.getElementById('setPwdBar');
  if(newPwd && bar && !newPwd.dataset.bound){ newPwd.dataset.bound='1';
    newPwd.addEventListener('input', () => {
      const v = newPwd.value; let score = 0;
      if(v.length > 0) score = 1;
      if(v.length >= 6) score = 2;
      if(v.length >= 10) score = 3;
      if(/[A-Za-z\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(v) && /\d/.test(v)) score = Math.max(score, 3);
      if(v.length >= 6 && /[A-Za-z\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(v) && /\d/.test(v)) score = 4;
      if(/[^A-Za-z0-9\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(v)) score = Math.min(4, score+1);
      bar.style.width = (score*25)+'%';
      bar.className = 'pw-bar s'+score;
    });
  }
}

    if(tR) tR.textContent = appSettings.reviewer.role;

    const spS = document.querySelector('.sp-sub'); if(spS) spS.textContent = appSettings.company.tagline;

    const i2 = document.getElementById('setReviewerRole'); if(i2) i2.value = appSettings.reviewer.role;

  } catch(e){ console.warn('applyAppSettingsToUI', e); }
}

function confirmFullImport(parsed){
  const d = parsed.data || {}, hasS = !!parsed.settings, hasSess = !!parsed.session, hasWS = Array.isArray(d.workSessions) && d.workSessions.length > 0;
  const meta = parsed.sessionsMeta || {};
  const dateStr = parsed.exportedAt ? new Date(parsed.exportedAt).toLocaleString('ar-SA') : '—';
  const s = parsed.settings || {};
  const importedIds = new Set((d.claims||[]).map(c=>c.id));
  const stale = hasWS ? d.workSessions.filter(w => !importedIds.has(w.claimId)).length : 0;
  const rec = hasWS ? d.workSessions.filter(w => !importedIds.has(w.claimId) && w.claimSnapshot && w.claimSnapshot.id).length : 0;
  openDialog({
    type:'warning', title:'استيراد كامل', message:'سيتم استبدال كل البيانات.',
    body: `
      <div style="background:#F8FAFC;border:1px solid #E2E8F0;border-radius:10px;padding:12px 16px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:2;text-align:right">
        <div><b>التاريخ:</b> ${escapeHtml(dateStr)}</div>
        <div><b>البيانات:</b> ${(d.claims||[]).length} مطالبة · ${(d.providers||[]).length} جهة · ${(d.contracts||[]).length} عقد · ${(d.tariffs||[]).length} لائحة</div>
        ${hasS?`<div style="color:var(--teal)"><b>✓ الإعدادات:</b> ${escapeHtml(s.reviewer?.name||'—')} — ${escapeHtml(s.company?.brand||'—')}</div>`:''}
        ${hasWS?`<div style="color:var(--info)"><b>✓ جلسات:</b> ${d.workSessions.length}${stale>0?` — <span style="color:var(--bad)">${stale} مفقودة</span>`:''}</div>`:''}
        ${hasSess?`<div style="color:var(--info)"><b>✓ حالة صفحة:</b> ${parsed.session?.currentPage||'—'}</div>`:''}
      </div>
      ${hasSess?`<label class="check-row" style="margin-top:10px;font-size:12px;background:#EFF6FF;border-color:#BFDBFE"><input type="checkbox" id="chkRSess" checked><span style="color:#1D4ED8;font-weight:700">حالة الصفحة</span></label>`:''}
      ${hasWS?`<label class="check-row" style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;background:#EFF6FF;border-color:#BFDBFE"><input type="checkbox" id="chkRWS" checked><span style="color:#1D4ED8;font-weight:700">جلسات (${d.workSessions.length})</span></label>
      <label class="check-row" style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;background:#F5F3FF;border-color:#DDD6FE"><input type="checkbox" id="chkRPtr" checked><span style="color:#6D28D9;font-weight:700">مؤشر الجلسة</span></label>
      ${rec>0?`<label class="check-row" style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;background:#FEF2F2;border-color:#FECACA"><input type="checkbox" id="chkRRec" checked><span style="color:#B91C1C;font-weight:700">استرداد ${rec}</span></label>`:''}`:''}
      <div style="margin-top:12px;font-size:11.5px;color:#92400E;background:#FEF3C7;border:1px solid #FCD34D;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;line-height:1.7">⚠️ سيتم حذف البيانات الحالية.</div>`,
    confirmText:'استيراد', cancelText:'إلغاء',
    onConfirm: () => applyFullBackup(parsed, {
      restoreSess: !!document.getElementById('chkRSess')?.checked,
      restoreSessions: !!document.getElementById('chkRWS')?.checked,
      restoreActivePtr: !!document.getElementById('chkRPtr')?.checked,
      recoverClaims: !!document.getElementById('chkRRec')?.checked
    })
  });
}
function applyFullBackup(parsed, opts){
  opts = opts || {};
  try {
    if(parsed.settings){
      if(parsed.settings.reviewer) appSettings.reviewer = { ...appSettings.reviewer, ...parsed.settings.reviewer };
      if(parsed.settings.company) appSettings.company = { ...appSettings.company, ...parsed.settings.company };
      saveAppSettings(); applyAppSettingsToUI();
    }
    const d = parsed.data || {};
    if(Array.isArray(d.providers)) providers = d.providers;
    if(Array.isArray(d.contracts)) contracts = d.contracts;
    if(Array.isArray(d.tariffs)) tariffs = d.tariffs;
    if(Array.isArray(d.batches)) batches = d.batches;
    if(Array.isArray(d.claims)) claims = d.claims;
    if(Array.isArray(d.trash)) trash = d.trash;
    if(Array.isArray(d.deductionRules)) deductionRules = d.deductionRules;
    if(Array.isArray(d.paProcedures)) paProcedures = d.paProcedures;
    claims.forEach(c => { if(!c.type) c.type = 'employee'; if(!c.invoices) c.invoices = []; if(!c.priorApproval) c.priorApproval = {required:false,status:'not-requested',refNumber:'',date:''}; recalcClaim(c); });
    activeSessionId = null;
    if(opts.restoreSessions && Array.isArray(d.workSessions)){
      if(document.getElementById('reviewModal')?.classList.contains('open')) try { closeReview({discard:true}); } catch(e){}
      let recovered = 0;
      if(opts.recoverClaims){
        const exIds = new Set(claims.map(c=>c.id));
        d.workSessions.forEach(w => { if(!exIds.has(w.claimId) && w.claimSnapshot && w.claimSnapshot.id){ const snap = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(w.claimSnapshot)); if(!snap.type) snap.type = 'employee'; if(!snap.invoices) snap.invoices = []; if(!snap.priorApproval) snap.priorApproval = {required:false,status:'not-requested',refNumber:'',date:''}; recalcClaim(snap); claims.push(snap); exIds.add(snap.id); recovered++; } });
      }
      workSessions = d.workSessions.map(w => ({ ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(w)), status:'paused' }));
      if(opts.restoreActivePtr && parsed.sessionsMeta?.activeSessionId && workSessions.some(s => s.id === parsed.sessionsMeta.activeSessionId)) activeSessionId = parsed.sessionsMeta.activeSessionId;
      if(parsed.sessionsMeta){ if(parsed.sessionsMeta.sessionsFilter !== undefined) sessionsFilter = parsed.sessionsMeta.sessionsFilter; if(typeof parsed.sessionsMeta.sessionsSearch === 'string') sessionsSearch = parsed.sessionsMeta.sessionsSearch; }
      persistSessions();
      if(recovered > 0) setTimeout(() => showToast(`استُردّت ${recovered}`), 600);
    }
    persistData(true);
    renderAll();
    if(opts.restoreSess && parsed.session) restoreSessionState(parsed.session);
    else navigateTo('dashboard', {noPush:true, force:true});
    const parts = [`${claims.length} مطالبة`, `${providers.length} جهة`];
    if(parsed.settings) parts.push('الإعدادات');
    if(opts.restoreSessions && Array.isArray(d.workSessions)) parts.push(`${workSessions.length} جلسة`);
    showSuccess('تم', parts.join(' + '));
  } catch(err){ console.error(err); showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
}
function confirmSettingsImport(parsed){
  const s = parsed.settings || {};
  openDialog({
    type:'warning', title:'استيراد الإعدادات', message:'سيتم استبدال الهوية والقواعد.',
    body: `<div style="background:#F8FAFC;border:1px solid #E2E8F0;border-radius:10px;padding:12px 16px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:2;text-align:right"><div><b>المراجع:</b> ${escapeHtml(s.reviewer?.name||'—')}</div><div><b>الشركة:</b> ${escapeHtml(s.company?.name||'—')}</div><div><b>قواعد:</b> ${(s.deductionRules||[]).length}</div><div><b>إجراءات:</b> ${(s.paProcedures||[]).length}</div></div>`,
    confirmText:'استيراد', cancelText:'إلغاء', onConfirm: () => applySettingsImport(parsed)
  });
}
function applySettingsImport(parsed){
  try {
    const s = parsed.settings || {};
    if(s.reviewer) appSettings.reviewer = { ...appSettings.reviewer, ...s.reviewer };
    if(s.company) appSettings.company = { ...appSettings.company, ...s.company };
    saveAppSettings(); applyAppSettingsToUI();
    if(Array.isArray(s.deductionRules)) deductionRules = s.deductionRules;
    if(Array.isArray(s.paProcedures)) paProcedures = s.paProcedures;
    persistData(true);
    renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تم');
  } catch(err){ showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
}
function confirmSessionsImport(parsed){
  const list = Array.isArray(parsed.sessions) ? parsed.sessions : [];
  const meta = parsed.sessionsMeta || {};
  const exIds = new Set(claims.map(c=>c.id));
  const stale = list.filter(w => !exIds.has(w.claimId)).length;
  const rec = list.filter(w => !exIds.has(w.claimId) && w.claimSnapshot && w.claimSnapshot.id).length;
  openDialog({
    type:'warning', title:'استيراد جلسات', message:'اختر الطريقة',
    body: `
      <div style="background:#F8FAFC;border:1px solid #E2E8F0;border-radius:10px;padding:12px 16px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:2;text-align:right;margin-bottom:12px">
        <div><b>عدد:</b> ${list.length}</div>
        ${stale>0?`<div style="color:var(--bad)"><b>⚠ مفقودة:</b> ${stale}${rec>0?` (${rec} قابلة)`:''}</div>`:''}
        <div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px;margin-top:4px">الحالية: ${workSessions.length}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        <label class="check-row" style="font-size:12px;background:#ECFDF5;border-color:#A7F3D0"><input type="radio" name="sessMode" value="merge" checked><span style="color:#047857;font-weight:800">دمج</span></label>
        <label class="check-row" style="font-size:12px;background:#FEF2F2;border-color:#FECACA"><input type="radio" name="sessMode" value="replace"><span style="color:#B91C1C;font-weight:800">استبدال</span></label>
      </div>
      ${rec>0?`<label class="check-row" style="margin-top:10px;font-size:12px;background:#EFF6FF;border-color:#BFDBFE"><input type="checkbox" id="chkSRec" checked><span style="color:#1D4ED8;font-weight:700">استرداد ${rec}</span></label>`:''}
      <label class="check-row" style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;background:#F5F3FF;border-color:#DDD6FE"><input type="checkbox" id="chkSPtr" checked><span style="color:#6D28D9;font-weight:700">مؤشر</span></label>`,
    confirmText:'استيراد', cancelText:'إلغاء',
    onConfirm: () => {
      const mode = document.querySelector('input[name="sessMode"]:checked')?.value || 'merge';
      const rec2 = !!document.getElementById('chkSRec')?.checked;
      const ptr = !!document.getElementById('chkSPtr')?.checked;
      applySessionsImport(parsed, { mode, recover:rec2, restorePtr:ptr });
    }
  });
}
function applySessionsImport(parsed, opts){
  opts = opts || {};
  try {
    const list = Array.isArray(parsed.sessions) ? parsed.sessions : [];
    if(!list.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','لا جلسات'); return; }
    if(document.getElementById('reviewModal')?.classList.contains('open')) try { closeReview({discard:true}); } catch(e){}
    let recovered = 0;
    if(opts.recover){
      const exIds = new Set(claims.map(c=>c.id));
      list.forEach(w => { if(!exIds.has(w.claimId) && w.claimSnapshot && w.claimSnapshot.id){ const snap = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(w.claimSnapshot)); if(!snap.type) snap.type = 'employee'; if(!snap.invoices) snap.invoices = []; if(!snap.priorApproval) snap.priorApproval = {required:false,status:'not-requested',refNumber:'',date:''}; recalcClaim(snap); claims.push(snap); exIds.add(snap.id); recovered++; } });
    }
    if(opts.mode === 'replace') workSessions = list.map(w => ({ ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(w)), status:'paused' }));
    else { const byId = new Map(workSessions.map(s => [s.id, s])); list.forEach(w => { byId.set(w.id, { ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(w)), status:'paused' }); }); workSessions = Array.from(byId.values()); }
    if(opts.restorePtr && parsed.sessionsMeta?.activeSessionId && workSessions.some(s => s.id === parsed.sessionsMeta.activeSessionId)) activeSessionId = parsed.sessionsMeta.activeSessionId;
    persistSessions(); persistData(true); renderSessions(); updateSessionsBadge();
    const msg = opts.mode === 'replace' ? `استبدال بـ ${workSessions.length}` : `دمج ${list.length} (إجمالي: ${workSessions.length})`;
    showSuccess('تم', msg + (recovered>0?` · ${recovered}`:''));
  } catch(err){ showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
}

function updateBackupStats(){
  const el = $('#backupStats');
  const n = (typeof workSessions !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(workSessions)) ? workSessions.length : 0;
  const lastBackup = getLastBackupAgo();
  if(el){
    el.innerHTML = `${claims.length} مطالبة · ${providers.length} جهة · ${contracts.length} عقد · ${tariffs.length} لائحة · ${n} جلسة · ${trash.length} محذوف<br>
      <span style="color:${lastBackup?'var(--ok)':'var(--warn)'};font-weight:700">💾 آخر نسخة: ${lastBackup || 'لم تُنشأ'}</span>`;
  }
  const st = document.getElementById('settingsTrashCount'); if(st) st.textContent = trash.length;
}

function syncActiveSessionBeforeExport(){
  try { if(activeClaim && document.getElementById('reviewModal') && document.getElementById('reviewModal').classList.contains('open')) upsertCurrentSession(); } catch(e){}
}
function getSessionState(){
  return {
    currentPage,
    viewState: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(viewState)),
    currentTimelineClaimId,
    filters: { currentFilter, currentSearch, dashSearchTerm, reportsSearchTerm, intakeMonthFilter, intakeStatusFilter, intakeSearch, providerStatusFilter, providerSearch, contractStatusFilter, contractSearch, tariffFilter, tariffSearch, drFilter, drSearch, trashFilter, trashSearch, srchTerm, srchTariffId, srchFilter, srchPage, srchPerPage, sessionsFilter, sessionsSearch },
    activeSessionId: activeSessionId || null,
    capturedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}
function restoreSessionState(state){
  if(!state) return;
  try {
    const f = state.filters || {};
    if(f.currentFilter !== undefined) currentFilter = f.currentFilter;
    if(typeof f.currentSearch === 'string') currentSearch = f.currentSearch;
    if(typeof f.dashSearchTerm === 'string') dashSearchTerm = f.dashSearchTerm;
    if(typeof f.reportsSearchTerm === 'string') reportsSearchTerm = f.reportsSearchTerm;
    if(f.intakeMonthFilter !== undefined) intakeMonthFilter = f.intakeMonthFilter;
    if(f.intakeStatusFilter !== undefined) intakeStatusFilter = f.intakeStatusFilter;
    if(typeof f.intakeSearch === 'string') intakeSearch = f.intakeSearch;
    if(f.providerStatusFilter !== undefined) providerStatusFilter = f.providerStatusFilter;
    if(typeof f.providerSearch === 'string') providerSearch = f.providerSearch;
    if(f.contractStatusFilter !== undefined) contractStatusFilter = f.contractStatusFilter;
    if(typeof f.contractSearch === 'string') contractSearch = f.contractSearch;
    if(f.tariffFilter !== undefined) tariffFilter = f.tariffFilter;
    if(typeof f.tariffSearch === 'string') tariffSearch = f.tariffSearch;
    if(f.drFilter !== undefined) drFilter = f.drFilter;
    if(typeof f.drSearch === 'string') drSearch = f.drSearch;
    if(f.trashFilter !== undefined) trashFilter = f.trashFilter;
    if(typeof f.trashSearch === 'string') trashSearch = f.trashSearch;
    if(typeof f.srchTerm === 'string') srchTerm = f.srchTerm;
    if(f.srchTariffId !== undefined) srchTariffId = f.srchTariffId;
    if(f.srchFilter !== undefined) srchFilter = f.srchFilter;
    if(typeof f.srchPage === 'number') srchPage = f.srchPage;
    if(typeof f.srchPerPage === 'number') srchPerPage = f.srchPerPage;
    if(f.sessionsFilter !== undefined) sessionsFilter = f.sessionsFilter;
    if(typeof f.sessionsSearch === 'string') sessionsSearch = f.sessionsSearch;
    if(state.currentTimelineClaimId) currentTimelineClaimId = state.currentTimelineClaimId;
    if(state.viewState && state.viewState.type) viewState = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.viewState));
    if(state.activeSessionId && workSessions.some(s => s.id === state.activeSessionId)) activeSessionId = state.activeSessionId;
    const syncChips = (sel, attr, val) => { const c = document.querySelector(sel); if(!c) return; c.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(ch => ch.classList.toggle('active', ch.dataset[attr] === val)); };
    syncChips('#filterChips','filter',currentFilter); syncChips('#intakeStatusChips','istatus',intakeStatusFilter);
    syncChips('#providerFilterChips','pfilter',providerStatusFilter); syncChips('#contractFilterChips','cfilter',contractStatusFilter);
    syncChips('#tariffFilterChips','tfilter',tariffFilter); syncChips('#drFilterChips','drf',drFilter);
    syncChips('#trashFilterChips','trf',trashFilter); syncChips('#srchChips','srch',srchFilter);
    syncChips('#sessionsFilterChips','sfilter',sessionsFilter);
    const mc = document.querySelector('#monthChips'); if(mc) mc.querySelectorAll('.month-chip').forEach(ch => ch.classList.toggle('active', ch.dataset.month === intakeMonthFilter));
    const setVal = (sel, v) => { const el = document.querySelector(sel); if(el) el.value = v; };
    setVal('#searchInput',currentSearch||''); setVal('#dashSearch',dashSearchTerm||''); setVal('#reportsSearch',reportsSearchTerm||'');
    setVal('#intakeSearch',intakeSearch||''); setVal('#providerSearch',providerSearch||''); setVal('#contractSearch',contractSearch||'');
    setVal('#tariffSearch',tariffSearch||''); setVal('#drSearch',drSearch||''); setVal('#trashSearch',trashSearch||'');
    setVal('#srchQuery',srchTerm||''); setVal('#srchTariff',srchTariffId||'all'); setVal('#srchPerPage',String(srchPerPage));
    setVal('#sessionsSearch',sessionsSearch||'');
    if(state.currentPage === 'view' && viewState.type && viewState.id){ applyPage('view'); renderView(); }
    else if(state.currentPage === 'timeline' && currentTimelineClaimId){ applyPage('timeline'); renderTimeline(); }
    else if(state.currentPage) applyPage(state.currentPage);
    if(typeof renderAll === 'function') renderAll();
    renderSessions(); updateSessionsBadge();
  } catch(e){ console.warn('restoreSessionState', e); }
}
function getBackupData(){
  syncActiveSessionBeforeExport();

    app:'TPA Pro', version:'1.0', type:'full-backup', exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    settings: { reviewer:{...appSettings.reviewer}, company:{...appSettings.company} },
    sessionsMeta: { totalCount:workSessions.length, activeCount:workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='active').length, pausedCount:workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='paused').length, activeSessionId:activeSessionId||null, sessionsFilter, sessionsSearch },
    data: { providers:providers.map(p=>({...p})), contracts:contracts.map(c=>({...c})), tariffs:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tariffs)), batches:batches.map(b=>({...b})), claims:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(claims)), trash:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(trash)), deductionRules:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(deductionRules)), paProcedures:paProcedures.map(p=>({...p})), workSessions:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(workSessions)) },
    session: getSessionState()
  };
}

    downloadJSON(getSessionsData(), `TPA_Pro_Sessions_${date}_${time}.json`);
    trackBackup();

  } catch(err){ showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
}
function doExportBackup(silent){
  syncActiveSessionBeforeExport();
  try {
    const now = new Date(), date = now.toISOString().slice(0,10), time = now.toTimeString().slice(0,5).replace(':','');
    downloadJSON(getBackupData(), `TPA_Pro_Backup_${date}_${time}.json`);
    trackBackup();
    if(!silent) showSuccess('تم', `${workSessions.length} جلسة`);
    return true;
  } catch(err){ if(!silent) showWarning('خطأ', err.message); return false; }
}

function exportSettings(){
  try { const d = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); downloadJSON(getSettingsData(), `TPA_Pro_Settings_${d}.json`); trackBackup(); showSuccess('تم','تم'); }
  catch(err){ showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
}
function importBackupFile(file){
  if(!file) return;
  if(file.size > 20 * 1024 * 1024){ showWarning('كبير','الحد 20MB'); return; }
  const r = new FileReader();
  r.onload = e => {
    try {
      const p = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
      if(!p || typeof p !== 'object'){ showWarning('غير صالح','ليس JSON'); return; }
      if(p.app !== 'TPA Pro'){ showWarning('غير صالح','ليس من TPA Pro'); return; }
      if(p.type === 'sessions') confirmSessionsImport(p);
      else if(p.type === 'settings') confirmSettingsImport(p);
      else if(p.type === 'full-backup' || p.data) confirmFullImport(p);
      else showWarning('غير معروف','—');
    } catch(err){ showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
  };
  r.onerror = () => showWarning('خطأ','تعذر');
  r.readAsText(file);
}

function applyMaxLabsRule(pat){
  markSessionDirty();
  const max = REVIEW_RULES.maxLabsPerVisit;
  const idxs = [];
  (pat.lines||[]).forEach((l,i) => { if(REVIEW_RULES.labCategories.includes(l.category)) idxs.push(i); });
  if(idxs.length <= max){ showWarning('لا حاجة', `عدد التحاليل ${idxs.length} ≤ ${max}.`); return; }
  const excess = idxs.length - max;
  const sorted = idxs.slice().sort((a,b) => (pat.lines[b].qty*pat.lines[b].billedUnit)-(pat.lines[a].qty*pat.lines[a].billedUnit));
  sorted.slice(0, excess).forEach(i => {
    const l = pat.lines[i];
    l.approved = 0;
    l.note = 'تجاوز حد التحاليل';
    l._maxlabsForced = true;
    l._overrideMode = 'auto';
    l._autoApproved = 0;
    l._autoRuleId = '_maxlabs';
    l._autoRuleName = `حد التحاليل (${max})`;
    l._appliedRuleIds = ['_maxlabs'];
  });
  showSuccess('تم', `تم خصم ${excess} تحليل`);
}
function saveReview(){
  if(!activeClaim) return;
  const missing = [];
  workingInvoices.forEach((inv,i) => { DOC_ITEMS.forEach(d => { if(!inv.documents[d.key]) missing.push(`فاتورة ${i+1}: ${d.label}`); }); });
  if(missing.length){ showWarning('مستندات ناقصة', missing.join('<br>')+'<br><br>متابعة؟', () => finalizeReview()); return; }
  if(activeClaim.priorApproval && activeClaim.priorApproval.required && activeClaim.priorApproval.status !== 'approved'){ showWarning('موافقة ناقصة','متابعة؟', () => finalizeReview()); return; }
  finalizeReview();
}
function finalizeReview(){
  if(!activeClaim) return;
  const cid = activeClaim.id;
  activeClaim.invoices = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(workingInvoices));
  activeClaim.invoices.forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv.date, inv)));
  activeClaim.reason = $('#rvReason').value;
  activeClaim.notes = $('#rvNotes').value.trim();
  if(activeClaim.priorApproval && activeClaim.priorApproval.required){
    activeClaim.priorApproval.status = $('#paStatus').value;
    activeClaim.priorApproval.refNumber = $('#paRefNumber').value.trim();
    activeClaim.priorApproval.date = $('#paDate').value;
  }
  recalcClaim(activeClaim);
  if(activeClaim.status === 'pending') activeClaim.reviewedDate = daysFromNow(0);
  if(activeClaim.approved <= 0) activeClaim.status = 'rejected';
  else if(activeClaim.approved >= activeClaim.billed) activeClaim.status = 'approved';
  else activeClaim.status = 'partial';
  workSessions = workSessions.filter(x => x.claimId !== activeClaim.id);
  persistSessions();
  clearSessionDirty();
  closeReview({ discard:true });
  renderAll();
  if(currentTimelineClaimId === cid) renderTimeline();
  showSuccess('تم','تم حفظ المراجعة');
}

function renderReviewInvoices(){
  if(!workingInvoices.length){
    $('#rvInvoiceTabs').innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="rv-add-tab" data-add-invoice="1">+ إضافة فاتورة</button>';
    $('#rvInvoicePanel').innerHTML = '<div class="rv-empty">لا فواتير</div>';
    recalcReview(); return;
  }
  $('#rvInvoiceTabs').innerHTML = workingInvoices.map((inv,i) => {
    const t = INVOICE_TYPES[inv.type] || {label:inv.type};
    const tot = invoiceTotals(inv);
    return `<button type="button" class="rv-inv-tab ${i===rvActiveInvoiceIdx?'active':''}" data-inv-idx="${i}"><span>${t.label}</span><span class="cnt">${(inv.patients||[]).length} مريض</span><span class="amt">${fmt(tot.billed)}</span></button>`;
  }).join('') + '<button type="button" class="rv-add-tab" data-add-invoice="1">+ فاتورة</button>';
  renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview();
}
function reviewLineRow(ln, i){
  const billed = ln.qty*ln.billedUnit;
  const hasT = ln.tariffUnit !== null && ln.tariffUnit !== undefined;
  const tt = hasT ? ln.qty*ln.tariffUnit : null;
  const lesser = ln.lineBase !== undefined ? ln.lineBase : (hasT ? Math.min(billed, tt) : billed);
  const appr = Number(ln.approved)||0;
  const diff = billed-appr;
  const mode = ln._overrideMode || 'auto';
  const flags = [];
  if(hasT && ln.billedUnit > ln.tariffUnit) flags.push('أعلى من اللائحة');
  if(hasT && ln.tariffUnit === 0) flags.push('غير مغطاة');
  if(ln.note) flags.push(ln.note);
  let status = '';
  if(ln._maxlabsForced) status = '<span class="rv-auto-badge auto">حد تحاليل</span>';
  else if(ln.paRejected) status = '<span class="rv-auto-badge rejected">مرفوض PA</span>';
  else if(mode === 'manual') status = '<span class="rv-auto-badge manual">يدوي</span>';
  else if(mode === 'reverted') status = '<span class="rv-auto-badge reverted">معاد</span>';
  else if(ln._compositeApplied && ln._compositeApplied.length) status = `<span class="rv-auto-badge auto">🎁 ${escapeHtml(ln._compositeApplied[0].name)}</span>`;
  else if(ln._autoRuleId) status = `<span class="rv-auto-badge auto">${ln._autoRuleRate}%${ln._appliedRuleIds&&ln._appliedRuleIds.length>1?' 🔗':''}</span>`;
  let rowCls = '';
  if(appr === 0) rowCls = 'rejected';
  else if(mode === 'manual') rowCls = 'flagged';
  else if(ln._autoRuleId && ln._autoApproved < ln.lineBase) rowCls = 'flagged';
  const hasAuto = (ln._appliedRuleIds && ln._appliedRuleIds.length > 0) && ln._autoApproved < ln.lineBase;
  const btns = `
    ${mode==='auto' && hasAuto ? `<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn undo" data-revert-line="${i}">↺</button>` : ''}
    ${mode!=='auto' ? `<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn reset" data-reset-line="${i}">⟲</button>` : ''}
    <button type="button" class="rv-line-act edit" data-edit-line="${i}">✎</button>
    <button type="button" class="rv-line-act del" data-del-line="${i}">×</button>`;
  const flagsHtml = flags.length ? ' · <span style="color:var(--warn);font-weight:700">' + flags.map(escapeHtml).join(' · ') + '</span>' : '';
  const autoRuleHtml = ln._autoRuleName ? ' · <span style="color:var(--info);font-weight:700">📋 ' + escapeHtml(ln._autoRuleName) + '</span>' : '';
  return `<tr class="${rowCls}">
    <td>${i+1}</td>
    <td><span class="mono" style="color:var(--navy-400);font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(ln.code)}</span></td>
    <td><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:12.5px">${escapeHtml(ln.name)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:1px">${escapeHtml(ln.category)}${flagsHtml}${autoRuleHtml}</div></td>
    <td class="mono">${ln.qty}</td>
    <td class="mono">${fmt(billed)}</td>
    <td class="mono">${hasT?fmt(tt):'—'}</td>
    <td class="mono" style="color:var(--teal);font-weight:800">${fmt(lesser)}</td>
    <td><input type="number" class="rv-line-approved" data-line-idx="${i}" value="${appr}" min="0" max="${billed}"></td>
    <td class="mono rv-diff" style="color:${diff>0?'var(--bad)':'var(--muted)'};font-weight:800">${fmt(diff)}</td>
    <td style="text-align:center">${status}</td>
    <td style="white-space:nowrap"><div style="display:flex;gap:2px">${btns}</div></td>
  </tr>`;
}
function renderActiveInvoice(){
  const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; const box = $('#rvInvoicePanel');
  if(!inv){ box.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  if(!inv.patients) inv.patients = [];
  if(rvActivePatientIdx >= inv.patients.length) rvActivePatientIdx = 0;
  const pat = inv.patients[rvActivePatientIdx];
  const t = INVOICE_TYPES[inv.type] || {label:inv.type};
  const tot = invoiceTotals(inv);
  const docs = DOC_ITEMS.map(d => { const on = !!inv.documents[d.key]; return `<label class="rv-doc ${on?'ok':'miss'}"><input type="checkbox" data-doc="${d.key}" ${on?'checked':''}>${d.label}</label>`; }).join('');
  const patTabs = inv.patients.map((p,i) => `<button type="button" class="rv-patient-tab ${i===rvActivePatientIdx?'active':''}" data-pat-idx="${i}"><span>${escapeHtml(p.name)}</span><span class="cnt">${(p.lines||[]).length}</span></button>`).join('') + '<button type="button" class="rv-add-tab" data-add-patient="1">+ مريض</button>';
  const rules = `<div class="rv-rule-bar"><button type="button" class="rv-rule-btn" data-rule="lesser">اعتماد الأقل</button><button type="button" class="rv-rule-btn" data-rule="billed">اعتماد كامل</button><button type="button" class="rv-rule-btn danger" data-rule="reject">رفض الكل</button><button type="button" class="rv-rule-btn warn" data-rule="maxlabs">حد التحاليل (${REVIEW_RULES.maxLabsPerVisit})</button></div>`;
  let patInfo = '', linesHtml = '';
  if(pat){
    computePatient(pat, activeClaim, inv.date, inv);
    const benType = patientBeneficiaryType(pat, activeClaim);
    const rej = (pat.lines||[]).filter(l=>l.paRejected).length;
    const capMode = pat._capMode || 'auto';
    const capLabel = capMode==='manual'?'يدوي':capMode==='reverted'?'معاد':(pat._autoCapRule?'تلقائي':'—');
    const capRow = pat._autoCapRule || capMode !== 'auto' ? `<div class="rv-pat-auto-row ${capMode==='manual'?'manual':(capMode==='reverted'?'reverted':'')}"><span class="lbl">🛡️ سقف (${pat._autoCapRule?pat._autoCapRule.rate+'%':''}) <span class="rv-auto-badge ${capMode==='manual'?'manual':capMode==='reverted'?'reverted':'auto'}">${capLabel}</span></span>${capMode==='manual'?`<input type="number" class="rv-manual-input" data-manual-cap="1" value="${pat._manualCapAmount||0}" min="0" max="${pat._subtotal}">`:`<span class="amt">${pat._capAmt?fmtC(pat._capAmt):'—'}</span><button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn" data-manual-cap-toggle="1" title="يدوي">✎</button>`}${pat._autoCapRule?`<span class="rule-name">${escapeHtml(pat._autoCapRule.name)}</span>`:''}${capMode==='auto'&&pat._autoCapAmt>0?`<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn undo" data-revert-cap="1">↺</button>`:''}${capMode!=='auto'?`<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn reset" data-reset-cap="1">⟲</button>`:''}</div>` : '';
    const copayMode = pat._copayMode || 'auto';
    const copayLabel = copayMode==='manual'?'يدوي':copayMode==='reverted'?'معاد':(pat._autoCopayRule?'تلقائي':'—');
    const copayRow = pat._autoCopayRule || copayMode !== 'auto' ? `<div class="rv-pat-auto-row ${copayMode==='manual'?'manual':(copayMode==='reverted'?'reverted':'')}"><span class="lbl">💰 تحمل (${pat._autoCopayRule?pat._autoCopayRule.rate+'%':''}) <span class="rv-auto-badge ${copayMode==='manual'?'manual':copayMode==='reverted'?'reverted':'auto'}">${copayLabel}</span></span>${copayMode==='manual'?`<input type="number" class="rv-manual-input" data-manual-copay="1" value="${pat._manualCopayAmount||0}" min="0" max="${pat._subtotal-pat._capAmt}">`:`<span class="amt">${pat._copayAmt?fmtC(pat._copayAmt):'—'}</span><button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn" data-manual-copay-toggle="1" title="يدوي">✎</button>`}${pat._autoCopayRule?`<span class="rule-name">${escapeHtml(pat._autoCopayRule.name)}</span>`:''}${copayMode==='auto'&&pat._autoCopayAmt>0?`<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn undo" data-revert-copay="1">↺</button>`:''}${copayMode!=='auto'?`<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn reset" data-reset-copay="1">⟲</button>`:''}</div>` : '';
    const autoBlock = (capRow||copayRow) ? `<div class="rv-pat-auto ${capMode==='manual'?'has-manual':pat._autoCapAmt>0?'has-auto':''}">${capRow}${copayRow}</div>` : '';
    patInfo = `<div class="rv-patient-info"><div class="rv-pinfo"><span>المريض</span><b>${escapeHtml(pat.name)}</b></div><div class="rv-pinfo"><span>الهوية</span><b>${escapeHtml(pat.nationalId||'—')}</b></div><div class="rv-pinfo"><span>النوع</span><b>${benType==='retiree'?'متقاعد':'عامل'}</b></div></div>
      <div class="rv-patient-calc"><div class="rv-pcalc-item"><span>إجمالي المريض</span><b>${fmtC(pat._subtotal)}</b></div><div class="rv-pcalc-item ded"><span>سقف</span><b>${pat._capAmt?fmtC(pat._capAmt):'—'}</b></div><div class="rv-pcalc-item ded"><span>تحمل</span><b>${pat._copayAmt?fmtC(pat._copayAmt):'—'}</b></div><div class="rv-pcalc-item ok"><span>المعتمد</span><b>${fmtC(pat._final)}</b></div></div>
      ${autoBlock}
      ${rej>0?`<div class="rv-pa-reject-note">⛔ ${rej} بند مرفوض — متقاعد بدون موافقة</div>`:''}`;
    linesHtml = pat.lines.length ? pat.lines.map((ln,i)=>reviewLineRow(ln,i)).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="11" style="text-align:center;padding:18px;color:var(--muted)">لا بنود</td></tr>';
  } else {
    patInfo = '<div class="rv-empty-patient">لا مرضى</div>';
    linesHtml = '<tr><td colspan="11" style="text-align:center;padding:18px;color:var(--muted)">—</td></tr>';
  }
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="rv-inv-meta">
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>النوع</span><b>${t.label}</b></div>
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>رقم</span><b>${escapeHtml(inv.number||inv.id)}</b></div>
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>التاريخ</span><b>${inv.date||'—'}</b></div>
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>${inv.type==='inpatient'?'الإقامة':'عدد المرضى'}</span><b>${inv.type==='inpatient'?(inv.admissionDate||'—')+' ← '+(inv.dischargeDate||'—'):inv.patients.length}</b></div>
    </div>
    <div class="rv-inv-actions">
      <button type="button" class="rv-act-btn primary" data-add-patient="1">مريض</button>
      <button type="button" class="rv-act-btn primary" data-add-line="1">بند</button>
      ${pat?`<button type="button" class="rv-act-btn ghost" data-edit-patient="1">تعديل المريض</button>`:''}
      <button type="button" class="rv-act-btn ghost" data-edit-invoice="1">تعديل الفاتورة</button>
      <button type="button" class="rv-act-btn danger" data-delete-invoice="1">حذف الفاتورة</button>
    </div>
    <div class="rv-inv-total"><span>مطالب: <b>${fmtC(tot.billed)}</b></span><span>معتمد: <b style="color:var(--ok)">${fmtC(tot.approved)}</b></span><span>استقطاع: <b style="color:var(--bad)">${fmtC(tot.deduction)}</b></span></div>
    <div class="rv-section-title">المستندات</div>
    <div class="rv-docs">${docs}</div>
    <div class="rv-section-title">المرضى</div>
    <div class="rv-patient-tabs">${patTabs}</div>
    ${patInfo}
    <div class="rv-section-title">بنود المريض</div>
    ${rules}
    <div class="rv-lines-wrap"><table class="rv-lines-table"><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>الكود</th><th>البند</th><th>كمية</th><th>فاتورة</th><th>لائحة</th><th>الأقل</th><th>معتمد</th><th>فرق</th><th>حالة</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${linesHtml}</tbody></table></div>
    <div class="rv-reapply-bar"><span>📋 القواعد نشطة</span><button type="button" data-reapply-all="1">⟲ إعادة تطبيق</button></div>`;
}
function recalcReview(){
  let billed = 0, eligible = 0, finalApproved = 0;
  workingInvoices.forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(pat => {
    computePatient(pat, activeClaim, inv.date, inv);
    billed += (pat.lines||[]).reduce((s,l)=>s + (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0), 0);
    eligible += pat._subtotal || 0;
    finalApproved += pat._final || 0;
  }));
  const ded = Math.max(0, billed-finalApproved);
  const rate = billed ? (ded/billed*100) : 0;
  $('#calcBilled').textContent = fmtC(billed);
  $('#calcDed').textContent = fmtC(ded);
  $('#calcRate').textContent = rate.toFixed(1)+'%';
}
function applyReviewRule(rule){
  markSessionDirty();
  const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; if(!inv||!inv.patients||!inv.patients.length) return;
  const pat = inv.patients[rvActivePatientIdx]; if(!pat) return;
  if(rule === 'maxlabs'){ applyMaxLabsRule(pat); renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }
  pat.lines.forEach(l => {
    const billed = l.qty*l.billedUnit;
    const hasT = l.tariffUnit !== null && l.tariffUnit !== undefined;
    const tt = hasT ? l.qty*l.tariffUnit : billed;
    if(rule === 'lesser'){ l.approved = Math.min(billed, tt); l._overrideMode='manual'; l._manualValue = l.approved; l.note=''; l._maxlabsForced = false; }
    else if(rule === 'billed'){ l.approved = billed; l._overrideMode='manual'; l._manualValue = billed; l.note=''; l._maxlabsForced = false; }
    else if(rule === 'reject'){ l.approved = 0; l._overrideMode='manual'; l._manualValue = 0; l._maxlabsForced = false; }
  });
  renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview();
}

================================================================================
FILE: /home/administrator/TPA/js/compute-patient.js
================================================================================
function computePatient(pat, claim, refDate, invoice){
  refDate = refDate || (invoice && invoice.date) || claim.date || claim.receivedDate || daysFromNow(0);
  const allRules = deductionRules.filter(r => r.enabled !== false);
  const ctx = { claim, patient: pat, invoice: invoice || null };
  (pat.lines||[]).forEach(l => {
    const billed = l.qty * l.billedUnit;
    const hasT = (l.tariffUnit !== null && l.tariffUnit !== undefined);
    const tariffTotal = hasT ? l.qty * l.tariffUnit : billed;
    const lesser = hasT ? Math.min(billed, tariffTotal) : billed;
    l.lineBase = lesser;
    if(l._overrideMode === undefined) l._overrideMode = 'auto';
  });
  const lineRules = topoSortRules(allRules.filter(r => r.isComposite || isLineLevelAction(r.action)));
  pat.lines.forEach(l => {
    const lctx = { ...ctx, line: l };
    const appliedIds = new Set();
    const compositeLog = [];
    let current = l.lineBase;
    let lastRuleId = null, lastRuleName = '', lastRuleRate = 0;
    for(const rule of lineRules){
      if(!checkRequires(rule, appliedIds)) continue;
      if(!ruleMatchesContext(rule, lctx, refDate)) continue;
      if(rule.isComposite){
        const cctx = { ...lctx, line: { ...l, lineBase: current } };
        const res = evaluateCompositeRule(rule, cctx, refDate);
        if(res && res.newApproved !== undefined && res.newApproved < current){
          current = res.newApproved;
          appliedIds.add(rule.id);
          (res.appliedChildRules||[]).forEach(cr => appliedIds.add(cr.id));
          compositeLog.push({ id: rule.id, name: rule.name, children: (res.appliedChildRules||[]).map(c=>c.id) });
          lastRuleId = rule.id; lastRuleName = rule.name; lastRuleRate = 0;
        }
      } else {
        const res = evaluateRuleAction(rule, lctx, current);
        if(res.newApproved !== undefined && res.newApproved < current){
          current = res.newApproved;
          appliedIds.add(rule.id);
          lastRuleId = rule.id; lastRuleName = rule.name; lastRuleRate = rule.rate;
        }
      }
    }
    l._autoApproved = current;
    l._appliedRuleIds = Array.from(appliedIds);
    l._autoRuleId = lastRuleId; l._autoRuleName = lastRuleName; l._autoRuleRate = lastRuleRate;
    l._compositeApplied = compositeLog;
    l.paRejected = false;
  });
  // ✅ F6: الأولوية لـ _maxlabsForced قبل القواعد العادية
  pat.lines.forEach(l => {
    if(l._maxlabsForced){
      l.approved = 0;
      l._autoApproved = 0;
      l._autoRuleId = '_maxlabs';
      l._autoRuleName = `حد التحاليل (${REVIEW_RULES.maxLabsPerVisit})`;
      l._autoRuleRate = 0;
      l._appliedRuleIds = ['_maxlabs'];
      l.note = 'تجاوز حد التحاليل';
      return;
    }
    if(l._overrideMode === 'manual' && l._manualValue !== null && l._manualValue !== undefined){
      l.approved = Math.max(0, Math.min(l.qty*l.billedUnit, Number(l._manualValue)||0));
    } else if(l._overrideMode === 'reverted'){ l.approved = l.lineBase; }
    else { l.approved = l._autoApproved; }
  });
  if(!pat.beneficiaryType) pat.beneficiaryType = claim.type || 'employee';
  const benType = patientBeneficiaryType(pat, claim);
  const paValid = hasValidPriorApproval(claim);
  if(benType === 'retiree' && !paValid){
    pat.lines.forEach(l => { if(lineRequiresPA(l)){ l.paRejected = true; l.approved = 0; } });
  }
  const subtotal = pat.lines.reduce((s,l)=>s+(Number(l.approved)||0), 0);
  const pctx = { ...ctx, patient: pat };
  const patientRules = topoSortRules(allRules.filter(r => ['apply_cap','apply_copay'].includes(r.action)));
  let autoCap = 0, capRule = null;
  let autoCopay = 0, copayRule = null;
  const appliedPids = new Set();
  for(const rule of patientRules.filter(r => r.action === 'apply_cap')){
    if(!checkRequires(rule, appliedPids)) continue;
    if(!ruleMatchesContext(rule, pctx, refDate)) continue;
    const r = evaluateRuleAction(rule, pctx, subtotal);
    if(r.deduct !== undefined && r.deduct > autoCap){ autoCap = r.deduct; capRule = rule; }
    appliedPids.add(rule.id);
  }
  for(const rule of patientRules.filter(r => r.action === 'apply_copay')){
    if(!checkRequires(rule, appliedPids)) continue;
    if(!ruleMatchesContext(rule, pctx, refDate)) continue;
    const r = evaluateRuleAction(rule, pctx, subtotal-autoCap);
    if(r.deduct !== undefined && r.deduct > autoCopay){ autoCopay = r.deduct; copayRule = rule; }
    appliedPids.add(rule.id);
  }
  let finalCap = autoCap, finalCopay = autoCopay;
  if(pat._capMode === 'manual' && pat._manualCapAmount !== null && pat._manualCapAmount !== undefined) finalCap = Number(pat._manualCapAmount)||0;
  else if(pat._capMode === 'reverted') finalCap = 0;
  if(pat._copayMode === 'manual' && pat._manualCopayAmount !== null && pat._manualCopayAmount !== undefined) finalCopay = Number(pat._manualCopayAmount)||0;
  else if(pat._copayMode === 'reverted') finalCopay = 0;
  pat._subtotal = subtotal;
  pat._autoCapAmt = autoCap; pat._autoCapRule = capRule;
  pat._autoCopayAmt = autoCopay; pat._autoCopayRule = copayRule;
  pat._capAmt = Math.max(0, Math.min(subtotal, Math.round(finalCap)));
  pat._copayAmt = Math.max(0, Math.min(subtotal-pat._capAmt, Math.round(finalCopay)));
  pat._final = Math.max(0, subtotal-pat._capAmt-pat._copayAmt);
  pat._beneficiaryPays = pat._capAmt + pat._copayAmt;
  pat._refDate = refDate;
  if(pat._capMode === undefined) pat._capMode = 'auto';
  if(pat._copayMode === undefined) pat._copayMode = 'auto';
  return pat;
}
function recalcClaim(c){
  if(!c.invoices) c.invoices = [];
  let billed = 0, eligible = 0, approved = 0, cap = 0, copay = 0;
  c.invoices.forEach(inv => {
    (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => {
      computePatient(p, c, inv.date, inv);
      billed += (p.lines||[]).reduce((s,l)=>s + (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0), 0);
      eligible += p._subtotal||0; approved += p._final||0;
      cap += p._capAmt||0; copay += p._copayAmt||0;
    });
  });
  c.billed = billed; c.eligible = eligible;
  c.approved = (c.status === 'pending') ? 0 : approved;
  c.violationDeduction = cap + copay;
  c.capDeduction = cap; c.copayDeduction = copay;
}
claims.forEach(c => { if(!c.type) c.type = 'employee'; recalcClaim(c); });

function mkLine(code, name, category, qty, billedUnit, tariffUnit, approved, note){
  const billed = qty * billedUnit;
  const hasT = (tariffUnit !== undefined && tariffUnit !== null);
  const tariffTotal = hasT ? qty * tariffUnit : null;
  const lesser = hasT ? Math.min(billed, tariffTotal) : billed;
  return { code, name, category, qty, billedUnit, tariffUnit: hasT ? tariffUnit : null,
           approved: (approved === undefined ? lesser : approved), note: note || '' };
}
function mkPatient(id, name, nationalId, lines){
  return { id, name: name || '—', nationalId: nationalId || '', lines: lines || [] };
}
function mkInvoice(id, type, date, patients, extra){
  return Object.assign({
    id, number: id, type, date,
    documents: { patientId:true, referral:true, medicalReport:true, priorApprovalDoc:true },
    admissionDate:'', dischargeDate:'', roomClass:'',
    patients: patients || []
  }, extra || {});
}
function allInvoiceLines(inv){
  if(!inv || !inv.patients) return [];
  return inv.patients.reduce((acc,p)=> acc.concat(p.lines || []), []);
}
function invoiceTotals(inv){
  const lines = allInvoiceLines(inv);
  const billed = lines.reduce((a,l)=> a + l.qty*l.billedUnit, 0);
  const approved = lines.reduce((a,l)=> a + (Number(l.approved)||0), 0);
  return { billed, approved, deduction: Math.max(0, billed-approved) };
}

function renderPaProcedures(){
  const body = $('#paProcBody'); if(!body) return;
  if(!paProcedures.length){ body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;padding:22px;color:var(--muted)">لا إجراءات</td></tr>'; return; }
  body.innerHTML = paProcedures.map(p => `<tr><td><span class="mono" style="color:var(--navy-400);font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(p.code)}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(p.name)}</td><td><span class="badge info">${escapeHtml(p.category)}</span></td><td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">${escapeHtml(p.notes||'—')}</td><td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:3px"><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-paproc="${escapeHtml(p.code)}">تحرير</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-del-paproc="${escapeHtml(p.code)}">×</button></div></td></tr>`).join('');
}
function openDrModal(id){
  editingDrId = id || null;
  const r = id ? deductionRules.find(x => x.id === id) : null;
  $('#drModalTitle').textContent = r ? 'تعديل' : 'إضافة';
  $('#drModalSub').textContent = r ? r.id : '—';
  $('#drName').value = r ? r.name : ''; $('#drType').value = r ? r.type : 'coverage_cap'; $('#drRate').value = r ? r.rate : 20;
  $('#drFrom').value = r ? r.effectiveFrom : daysFromNow(0); $('#drTo').value = r ? (r.effectiveTo||'') : daysFromNow(365);
  $('#drActive').checked = r ? r.active : true; $('#drNotes').value = r ? r.notes||'' : '';
  const ct = r ? (r.scope.claimTypes||[]) : ['employee'];
  $$('.dr-ctype').forEach(cb => cb.checked = ct.includes(cb.value));
  (el => { if (el) el.style.display = r ? 'block' : 'none'; })($('#deleteDrBtn'));
  $('#drModal').classList.add('open');
}
function closeDrModal(){ $('#drModal').classList.remove('open'); editingDrId = null; }
function saveDr(){
  const name = $('#drName').value.trim(), type = $('#drType').value, rate = Number($('#drRate').value);
  const from = $('#drFrom').value, to = $('#drTo').value;
  if(!name){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الاسم'); return; }
  if(isNaN(rate) || rate < 0 || rate > 100){ showWarning('تنبيه','النسبة'); return; }
  if(!from){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل السريان'); return; }
  const cts = $$('.dr-ctype').filter(cb=>cb.checked).map(cb=>cb.value);
  if(!cts.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','حدد نوع'); return; }
  const data = { name, type, action: type==='coverage_cap'?'apply_cap':'apply_copay', rate, effectiveFrom:from, effectiveTo:to||'', active:$('#drActive').checked, notes:$('#drNotes').value.trim(), scope:{ providerIds:[], contractIds:[], claimTypes:cts } };
  if(editingDrId){ const r = deductionRules.find(x=>x.id===editingDrId); if(r) Object.assign(r, data); showSuccess('تم','تم'); }
  else {
    const y = new Date(from).getFullYear(), pre = 'DR-'+y+'-';
    let m = 0;
    deductionRules.forEach(x => { if(x.id.startsWith(pre)){ const n = parseInt(x.id.split('-').pop(), 10); if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m,n); } });
    deductionRules.push({ id:pre+String(m+1).padStart(3,'0'), ...data });
    showSuccess('تم','تمت');
  }
  closeDrModal(); renderDeductionRules();
}
function deleteDr(id){
  const r = deductionRules.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!r) return;
  showDelete('حذف', `"${r.name}".`, () => { deductionRules = deductionRules.filter(x=>x.id!==id); closeDrModal(); renderDeductionRules(); showSuccess('تم','تم'); });
}
function openPaProcModal(code){
  editingPaProcCode = code || null;
  const p = code ? paProcedures.find(x=>x.code===code) : null;
  $('#paProcModalTitle').textContent = p ? 'تعديل' : 'إضافة';
  $('#paProcCode').value = p ? p.code : ''; $('#paProcName').value = p ? p.name : '';
  $('#paProcCat').value = p ? p.category : 'عمليات'; $('#paProcNotes').value = p ? p.notes||'' : '';
  $('#paProcModal').classList.add('open');
}
function savePaProc(){
  const code = $('#paProcCode').value.trim(), name = $('#paProcName').value.trim();
  if(!code || !name){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الكود والاسم'); return; }
  const data = { code, name, category:$('#paProcCat').value, notes:$('#paProcNotes').value.trim() };
  if(editingPaProcCode){ const i = paProcedures.findIndex(x=>x.code===editingPaProcCode); if(i>=0) paProcedures[i] = data; }
  else { if(paProcedures.find(x=>x.code===code)){ showWarning('تنبيه','مستخدم'); return; } paProcedures.push(data); }
  $('#paProcModal').classList.remove('open'); editingPaProcCode = null;
  renderPaProcedures(); renderDeductionRules(); showSuccess('تم','تم');
}

function testDeductionRule(id){
  const rule = deductionRules.find(r => r.id === id);
  if(!rule){ showWarning('تنبيه','القاعدة غير موجودة'); return; }
  if(!claims.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','لا مطالبات للاختبار'); return; }
  const claim = claims[0];
  const inv0 = claim.invoices && claim.invoices[0];
  const pat = inv0 && inv0.patients && inv0.patients[0];
  if(!pat || !pat.lines || !pat.lines.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','لا بنود'); return; }
  const line = pat.lines[0];
  const inv = claim.invoices && claim.invoices[0];
  const ctx = { claim, patient: pat, invoice: inv, line: { ...line, lineBase: line.qty * line.billedUnit } };
  ctx.line.lineBase = line.qty * (line.tariffUnit != null ? Math.min(line.billedUnit, line.tariffUnit) : line.billedUnit);
  const refDate = claim.date || claim.receivedDate || daysFromNow(0);
  const matched = ruleMatchesContext(rule, ctx, refDate);
  const res = evaluateRuleAction(rule, ctx, ctx.line.lineBase);
  const msg = `القاعدة: ${escapeHtml(rule.name)}<br>مطابقة: ${matched ? '✅ نعم' : '❌ لا'}<br>الأساس: ${fmt(ctx.line.lineBase)}<br>النتيجة: ${res.newApproved !== undefined ? fmt(res.newApproved) : fmt(ctx.line.lineBase - (res.deduct||0))}<br>الخصم: ${fmt(res.deduct || (ctx.line.lineBase - (res.newApproved||ctx.line.lineBase)))}`;
  showInfo('اختبار قاعدة', msg);
}

let drFilter = 'all', drSearch = '', editingDrId = null;
let editingPaProcCode = null;
function bindDeductionRulesControls(){
  $$('.dr-tab').forEach(t => t.addEventListener('click', () => {
    $$('.dr-tab').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
    t.classList.add('active');
    $$('.dr-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.toggle('active', p.dataset.drPanel === t.dataset.drTab));
  }));
  $('#drSearch').addEventListener('input', e => { drSearch = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); renderDeductionRules(); });
  $('#drFilterChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#drFilterChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); drFilter = chip.dataset.drf; renderDeductionRules();
  });
  $('#addDrBtn').addEventListener('click', () => openDrWizard());
  $('#drBody').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const ed = e.target.closest('[data-edit-dr]'); if(ed) openDrModal(ed.dataset.editDr);
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-del-dr]'); if(del) deleteDr(del.dataset.delDr);
    const ts = e.target.closest('[data-test-dr]'); if(ts) testDeductionRule(ts.dataset.testDr);
  });
  $$('[data-close-dr]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', closeDrModal));
  $('#saveDrBtn').addEventListener('click', saveDr);
  $('#deleteDrBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { if(editingDrId) deleteDr(editingDrId); });
  $('#addPaProcBtn').addEventListener('click', () => openPaProcModal());
  $('#paProcBody').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const ed = e.target.closest('[data-edit-paproc]'); if(ed) openPaProcModal(ed.dataset.editPaproc);
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-del-paproc]'); if(del){ paProcedures = paProcedures.filter(x => x.code !== del.dataset.delPaproc); renderPaProcedures(); showSuccess('تم','تم'); }
  });
  $$('[data-close-paproc]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => $('#paProcModal').classList.remove('open')));
  $('#savePaProcBtn').addEventListener('click', savePaProc);
  bindDrWizard();
}
function drStatus(r){
  const today = daysFromNow(0);
  if(!r.active) return { label:'معطّلة', cls:'inactive' };
  if(r.effectiveFrom && today < r.effectiveFrom) return { label:'لم تبدأ', cls:'upcoming' };
  if(r.effectiveTo && today > r.effectiveTo) return { label:'منتهية', cls:'expired' };
  return { label:'نشطة', cls:'active' };
}
function renderDeductionRules(){
  const activeN = deductionRules.filter(r => drStatus(r).cls === 'active').length;
  const capN = deductionRules.filter(r => r.type === 'coverage_cap').length;
  const copayN = deductionRules.filter(r => r.type === 'retiree_copay').length;
  $('#drKpi').innerHTML = `
    <div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">القواعد</div><div class="kpi-val">${deductionRules.length} <small>قاعدة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('shield')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${activeN} نشطة</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k2"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">سقف</div><div class="kpi-val">${capN} <small>قاعدة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('file')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">للعاملين</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k3"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">تحمل</div><div class="kpi-val">${copayN} <small>قاعدة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('money')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">للمعاشات</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k4"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">إجراءات</div><div class="kpi-val">${paProcedures.length} <small>إجراء</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('contract')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">الموافقة</div></div>`;
  const list = deductionRules.filter(r => {
    if(drFilter === 'active' && drStatus(r).cls !== 'active') return false;
    if(drFilter === 'expired' && drStatus(r).cls !== 'expired') return false;
    if((drFilter === 'coverage_cap' || drFilter === 'retiree_copay') && r.type !== drFilter) return false;
    if(drSearch){ const q = drSearch; return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.type.includes(q); }
    return true;
  });
  const body = $('#drBody');
  if(!list.length) body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:26px;color:var(--muted)">لا قواعد</td></tr>';
  else body.innerHTML = list.map(r => {
    const st = drStatus(r);
    const tl = r.type === 'coverage_cap' ? 'سقف' : r.type === 'retiree_copay' ? 'تحمل' : r.isComposite ? 'سوبر' : 'مخصص';
    const tc = r.type === 'coverage_cap' ? 'cap' : 'copay';
    const tags = [];
    if(r.scope?.providerIds?.length) tags.push(`<span class="dr-scope-tag">${r.scope.providerIds.length} جهة</span>`);
    (r.scope?.claimTypes||[]).forEach(ct => tags.push(`<span class="dr-scope-tag ${ct==='retiree'?'warn':''}">${ct==='employee'?'عاملين':'معاشات'}</span>`));
    if(!r.scope?.providerIds?.length) tags.unshift('<span class="dr-scope-tag bad">كل</span>');
    return `<tr>
      <td data-label="الرقم"><span class="clm-id">${escapeHtml(r.id)}</span></td>
      <td data-label="الاسم"><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:12.5px">${escapeHtml(r.name)}</div>${r.notes?`<div style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px">${escapeHtml(r.notes)}</div>`:''}</td>
      <td data-label="النوع"><span class="dr-type-badge ${tc}">${tl}</span></td>
      <td data-label="النسبة"><span class="dr-rate-badge">${r.rate}%</span></td>
      <td data-label="النطاق"><div class="dr-scope-tags">${tags.join('')}</div></td>
      <td data-label="السريان" style="font-size:11px;line-height:1.6"><div>${escapeHtml(r.effectiveFrom||'—')}</div><div style="color:var(--muted)">${escapeHtml(r.effectiveTo||'—')}</div></td>
      <td data-label="الحالة"><span class="dr-status-pill ${st.cls}">${st.label}</span></td>
      <td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:3px"><button class="btn-xs" data-test-dr="${escapeHtml(r.id)}">اختبار</button><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-dr="${escapeHtml(r.id)}">تحرير</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-del-dr="${escapeHtml(r.id)}">×</button></div></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
  renderPaProcedures();
}

}

function compareValues(actual, op, exp, ctx){
  ctx = ctx || {};
  if(isDateOperator(op)) return compareDates(actual, op, exp, ctx);
  if(op === 'len_eq') return String(actual||'').length === parseNumber(exp);
  if(op === 'len_gt') return String(actual||'').length > parseNumber(exp);
  if(op === 'len_lt') return String(actual||'').length < parseNumber(exp);
  if(op === 'normalize_eq') return normalizeArabic(actual) === normalizeArabic(exp);
  if(op === 'normalize_contains') return normalizeArabic(actual).includes(normalizeArabic(exp));
  if(op === 'regex_match'){ try { return new RegExp(String(exp)).test(String(actual||'')); } catch(e){ return false; } }
  if(op === 'similar_to'){ const [t, th] = String(exp||'').split('|'); return levenshteinSimilarity(String(actual||''), t||'') >= (parseNumber(th)||80); }
  if(op === 'not_contains') return !String(actual||'').includes(String(exp||''));
  if(op === 'num_between'){ const [lo, hi] = String(exp||'').split(',').map(parseNumber); return parseNumber(actual)>=lo && parseNumber(actual)<=hi; }
  if(op === 'approx_eq'){ const [t, tol] = String(exp||'').split('|'); return Math.abs(parseNumber(actual)-parseNumber(t)) <= (parseNumber(tol)||0.5); }
  if(['gt','gte','lt','lte'].includes(op)){
    const a = parseNumber(actual), b = parseNumber(exp);
    if(String(actual).trim()!=='' && String(exp).trim()!==''){
      if(op === 'gt') return a > b;
      if(op === 'gte') return a >= b;
      if(op === 'lt') return a < b;
      if(op === 'lte') return a <= b;
    }
  }
  switch(op){
    case 'eq': return String(actual) === String(exp);
    case 'ne': return String(actual) !== String(exp);
    case 'in': { const arr = String(exp||'').split(',').map(s=>s.trim()); return arr.includes(String(actual)); }
    case 'not_in': { const arr = String(exp||'').split(',').map(s=>s.trim()); return !arr.includes(String(actual)); }
    case 'contains': return String(actual||'').includes(String(exp||''));
    case 'starts_with': return String(actual||'').startsWith(String(exp||''));
    case 'exists': return actual !== undefined && actual !== null;
    case 'is_empty': return !actual || String(actual).trim() === '';
    case 'is_not_empty': return actual !== undefined && actual !== null && String(actual).trim() !== '';
    case 'is_true': return actual === true || actual === 'true';
    case 'is_false': return actual === false || actual === 'false';
    default: return true;
  }
}
function getScopeItems(scope, ctx){
  if(scope === 'self' || scope === 'line') return ctx.line ? [{line:ctx.line, patient:ctx.patient, invoice:ctx.invoice, claim:ctx.claim}] : [];
  if(scope === 'patient') return (ctx.patient?.lines||[]).map(l=>({line:l, patient:ctx.patient, invoice:ctx.invoice, claim:ctx.claim}));
  if(scope === 'invoice') return (ctx.invoice?.patients||[]).flatMap(pt=>(pt.lines||[]).map(l=>({line:l, patient:pt, invoice:ctx.invoice, claim:ctx.claim})));
  if(scope === 'claim') return (ctx.claim?.invoices||[]).flatMap(iv=>(iv.patients||[]).flatMap(pt=>(pt.lines||[]).map(l=>({line:l, patient:pt, invoice:iv, claim:ctx.claim}))));
  return [];
}
function evaluateCondition(cond, ctx){
  if(!cond) return true;
  const mode = cond.mode || 'expr';
  const scope = cond.scope || 'self';
  if(mode === 'expr'){ if(!cond.field || !cond.op) return true; return compareValues(resolveFieldPath(cond.field, ctx), cond.op, cond.value, ctx); }
  if(mode === 'duplicate' || mode === 'unique'){
    const f = cond.field || 'line.code';
    const items = getScopeItems(scope, ctx);
    const my = resolveFieldPath(f, ctx);
    const cnt = items.filter(it => String(resolveFieldPath(f, it)) === String(my)).length;
    return mode === 'duplicate' ? cnt > 1 : cnt === 1;
  }
  const items = getScopeItems(scope, ctx);
  if(mode === 'any'){ if(!cond.subField || !cond.subOp) return true; return items.some(it => compareValues(resolveFieldPath(cond.subField, it), cond.subOp, cond.subValue, it)); }
  if(mode === 'all'){ if(!cond.subField || !cond.subOp) return true; if(!items.length) return false; return items.every(it => compareValues(resolveFieldPath(cond.subField, it), cond.subOp, cond.subValue, it)); }
  if(mode === 'count'){ if(!cond.subField || !cond.subOp) return true; const n = items.filter(it => compareValues(resolveFieldPath(cond.subField, it), cond.subOp, cond.subValue, it)).length; return compareValues(n, cond.compareOp||'gt', cond.compareValue||0, ctx); }
  if(['sum','avg','max','min'].includes(mode)){
    const vals = items.map(it => Number(resolveFieldPath(cond.aggField||'line.billedTotal', it))||0);
    let res = 0;
    if(mode === 'sum') res = vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
    else if(mode === 'avg') res = vals.length ? vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/vals.length : 0;
    else if(mode === 'max') res = vals.length ? Math.max(...vals) : 0;
    else if(mode === 'min') res = vals.length ? Math.min(...vals) : 0;
    return compareValues(Math.round(res), cond.compareOp||'gt', cond.compareValue||0, ctx);
  }
  return true;
}
function evaluateConditionTree(node, ctx){
  if(!node || !node.rules || !node.rules.length) return true;
  const res = node.rules.map(item => {
    if(item.logic && Array.isArray(item.rules)) return evaluateConditionTree(item, ctx);
    return evaluateCondition(item, ctx);
  });
  return node.logic === 'OR' ? res.some(Boolean) : res.every(Boolean);
}
