function openDrWizard(){
  wizState = { step:1, type:null, name:'', rate:0, from:'', to:'', notes:'', ctypes:['employee'], providerIds:[], contractIds:[], action:'apply_cap', conditions:{logic:'AND',rules:[]}, requires:{mode:'any',ruleIds:[]}, compositeConfig:{childRuleIds:[],applyMode:'atomic',requireAllMatch:true}, tiers:[], tiersEnabled:false };
  $('#drWizardModal').classList.add('open'); wizRenderStep();
}
function closeDrWizard(){ $('#drWizardModal').classList.remove('open'); }
function bindDrWizard(){
  $$('[data-close-drwiz]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', closeDrWizard));
  $('#wizBackBtn').addEventListener('click', wizBack);
  $('#wizNextBtn').addEventListener('click', wizNext);
  $$('[data-wiz-jump]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => wizGoTo(Number(el.dataset.wizJump))));
  $$('.wiz-type-card').forEach(card => card.addEventListener('click', () => {
    wizState.type = card.dataset.wizType;
    if(wizState.type === 'coverage_cap'){ wizState.action = 'apply_cap'; if(!wizState.ctypes.includes('employee')) wizState.ctypes.push('employee'); }
    else if(wizState.type === 'retiree_copay'){ wizState.action = 'apply_copay'; if(!wizState.ctypes.includes('retiree')) wizState.ctypes.push('retiree'); }
    else if(wizState.type === 'composite') wizState.action = 'composite';
    $$('.wiz-type-card').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('active', c === card));
  }));
  $('#wizAction').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.action = e.target.value; });
  $('#wizCompositeMode').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.compositeConfig.applyMode = e.target.value; });
  $('#wizRequireAll').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.compositeConfig.requireAllMatch = e.target.checked; });
  $('#wizRequiresMode').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.requires.mode = e.target.value; });
  $('#wizRequiresClear').addEventListener('click', () => { wizState.requires.ruleIds = []; wizRenderRequires(); });
  $('#wizRatePresets').addEventListener('click', e => { const btn = e.target.closest('[data-wiz-rate]'); if(!btn) return; wizState.rate = Number(btn.dataset.wizRate); $('#wizRate').value = wizState.rate; });
  $('#wizPeriodPresets').addEventListener('click', e => { const btn = e.target.closest('[data-wiz-period]'); if(!btn) return; const p = WIZ_PERIOD_PRESETS[Number(btn.dataset.wizPeriod)]; wizState.from = p.from(); wizState.to = p.to(); $('#wizFrom').value = wizState.from; $('#wizTo').value = wizState.to; });
  $$('.wiz-ctype').forEach(cb => cb.addEventListener('change', () => { if(cb.checked){ if(!wizState.ctypes.includes(cb.value)) wizState.ctypes.push(cb.value); } else wizState.ctypes = wizState.ctypes.filter(x=>x!==cb.value); cb.closest('.scope-chk').classList.toggle('checked', cb.checked); }));
  $('#condLogic').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.conditions.logic = e.target.value; wizRenderCondPreview(); });
  $('#addCondBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { wizState.conditions.rules.push({ field:'line.category', op:'eq', value:'مختبر' }); wizRenderConditions(); });
  $('#addGroupBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { wizState.conditions.rules.push({ logic:'OR', rules:[{ field:'line.billedUnit', op:'gt', value:500 }] }); wizRenderConditions(); });
  $('#drWizardModal').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const sa = e.target.closest('[data-wiz-selectall]');
    if(sa){ if(sa.dataset.wizSelectall === 'providers') wizState.providerIds = providers.map(p=>p.id); else wizState.contractIds = contracts.map(c=>c.id); wizRenderScope(); }
    const cl = e.target.closest('[data-wiz-clear]');
    if(cl){ if(cl.dataset.wizClear === 'providers') wizState.providerIds = []; else wizState.contractIds = []; wizRenderScope(); }
  });
  const addTierBtn = $('#wizAddTierBtn');
  if(addTierBtn) addTierBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { if(!wizState.tiers) wizState.tiers = []; wizState.tiers.push({from:0, to:null, rate:0}); wizState.tiersEnabled = true; const te = $('#wizTiersEnable'); if(te) te.checked = true; wizRenderTiers(); });
  const tiersEnable = $('#wizTiersEnable');
  if(tiersEnable) tiersEnable.addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.tiersEnabled = e.target.checked; if(!wizState.tiersEnabled) wizState.tiers = []; });
}

function wizConditionRowHTML(cond, idPrefix, inGroup, parentPath){
  parentPath = parentPath || '';
  const mode = cond.mode || 'expr';
  const scope = cond.scope || 'self';
  const scopeSel = inGroup ? '' : `<select class="sel-scope" data-cond-scope="${idPrefix}">${Object.entries(WIZ_SCOPES).map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${k}" ${scope===k?'selected':''}>${v.label}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
  const modeSel = inGroup ? '' : `<select class="sel-mode" data-cond-mode="${idPrefix}">${Object.entries(WIZ_MODES).map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${k}" ${mode===k?'selected':''}>${v.label}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
  const fSel = val => `<select data-cond-field="${idPrefix}">${WIZ_FIELDS.map(x=>`<option value="${x.path}" ${val===x.path?'selected':''}>${x.label}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
  const opOptions = (val) => WIZ_OP_GROUPS.map(g => `<optgroup label="${g.label}">${g.ops.map(k=>`<option value="${k}" ${val===k?'selected':''}>${(WIZ_OPS[k]||{}).label||k}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>`).join('') + `<optgroup label="أخرى">${Object.keys(WIZ_OPS).filter(k=>!WIZ_OP_GROUPS.some(g=>g.ops.includes(k))).map(k=>`<option value="${k}" ${val===k?'selected':''}>${WIZ_OPS[k].label}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>`;
  const oSel = (val,attr) => `<select data-cond-${attr}="${idPrefix}">${opOptions(val)}</select>`;
  const vInp = (val,attr,opKey) => { const o = cond[opKey]; if((WIZ_OPS[o]||{}).noValue) return ''; return `<input type="text" data-cond-${attr}="${idPrefix}" value="${val||''}" placeholder="قيمة">`; };
  let inner = '';
  if(mode === 'expr') inner = fSel(cond.field||'line.category') + oSel(cond.op||'eq','op') + vInp(cond.value,'value','op');
  else if(mode === 'any' || mode === 'all') inner = fSel(cond.subField||'line.code') + oSel(cond.subOp||'eq','subOp') + vInp(cond.subValue,'subValue','subOp');
  else if(mode === 'count') inner = fSel(cond.subField||'line.category') + oSel(cond.subOp||'eq','subOp') + vInp(cond.subValue,'subValue','subOp') + oSel(cond.compareOp||'gt','compareOp') + `<input type="number" data-cond-compareValue="${idPrefix}" value="${cond.compareValue||0}">`;
  else if(['sum','avg','max','min'].includes(mode)) inner = fSel(cond.aggField||'line.billedTotal') + oSel(cond.compareOp||'gt','compareOp') + `<input type="number" data-cond-compareValue="${idPrefix}" value="${cond.compareValue||0}">`;
  else if(mode === 'duplicate' || mode === 'unique') inner = fSel(cond.field||'line.code');
  return `<div class="cond-item mode-${mode}" data-drag-path="${idPrefix}" data-drop-container="${parentPath}">
    <span class="cond-drag-handle" draggable="true" aria-grabbed="false" tabindex="0" title="سحب">⠿</span>
    ${scopeSel}${modeSel}${inner}
    <div class="cond-reorder-btns">
      <button type="button" data-move-up="${idPrefix}" title="أعلى">▲</button>
      <button type="button" data-move-down="${idPrefix}" title="أسفل">▼</button>
    </div>
    <button type="button" class="cond-del" data-cond-del="${idPrefix}">×</button>
  </div>`;
}
function renderNodeHTML(node, path, parentPath){
  parentPath = parentPath || '';
  if(node && node.logic && Array.isArray(node.rules)){
    const inner = node.rules.map((ic,iidx) => renderNodeHTML(ic, path + '_c' + iidx, path)).join('');
    return `<div class="cond-group" data-drag-path="${path}" data-drop-into="${path}" data-drop-container="${parentPath}">
      <div class="cond-group-head">
        <span class="cond-drag-handle" draggable="true" tabindex="0" title="سحب">⠿</span>
        <span>مجموعة</span>
        <select data-group-logic="${path}" class="cond-logic-sel" style="height:26px;font-size:10.5px"><option value="AND" ${node.logic==='AND'?'selected':''}>ALL</option><option value="OR" ${node.logic==='OR'?'selected':''}>ANY</option></select>
        <button type="button" class="del-grp" data-group-del="${path}">×</button>
        <div class="cond-reorder-btns">
          <button type="button" data-move-up="${path}" title="أعلى">▲</button>
          <button type="button" data-move-down="${path}" title="أسفل">▼</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="cond-list" data-drop-container="${path}" data-drop-target-end="true">${inner}</div>
      <div class="cond-actions">
        <button type="button" class="btn-apply out" data-group-add-cond="${path}" style="height:26px;padding:0 10px;font-size:10.5px;margin-top:4px">+ شرط</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn-apply out" data-group-add-group="${path}" style="height:26px;padding:0 10px;font-size:10.5px;margin-top:4px;margin-right:4px">+ مجموعة</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }
  return wizConditionRowHTML(node, path, false, parentPath);
}
function wizRenderConditions(){
  const list = $('#condList'); const conds = wizState.conditions.rules || [];
  if(!conds.length) list.innerHTML = '<div class="cond-empty">لا شروط</div>';
  else list.innerHTML = conds.map((c,idx) => renderNodeHTML(c, (c.logic&&Array.isArray(c.rules)) ? 'g'+idx : 'c'+idx, '')).join('');
  wizRenderCondPreview(); wizBindConditionHandlers(); if(typeof initDragDrop === 'function') initDragDrop();
}
function wizRenderCondPreview(){
  const el = $('#condPreview'); if(!el) return;
  const conds = wizState.conditions.rules || [];
  if(!conds.length){ el.classList.remove('show'); return; }
  el.innerHTML = `سيتم التطبيق عند: ${conds.length} شرط`;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function parsePath(id){
  if(!id) return null;
  const nums = String(id).match(/\d+/g);
  if(!nums) return null;
  return nums.map(Number);
}
function getNodeByPath(path){ let node = { rules: wizState.conditions.rules }; for(const i of path){ if(!node.rules || !node.rules[i]) return null; node = node.rules[i]; } return node; }
function getParentByPath(path){ if(path.length === 1) return { parent: { rules: wizState.conditions.rules }, idx: path[0] }; const p = getNodeByPath(path.slice(0,-1)); return p ? { parent: p, idx: path[path.length-1] } : null; }
function wizBindConditionHandlers(){
  const root = $('#condList');
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-scope]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condScope); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.scope = e.target.value; n.field = null; n.op = null; n.value = null; wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-mode]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condMode); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.mode = e.target.value; n.field = null; n.op = null; n.value = null; wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-field]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condField); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; const mode = n.mode || 'expr'; if(mode === 'expr' || mode === 'duplicate' || mode === 'unique') n.field = e.target.value; else if(mode === 'any' || mode === 'all' || mode === 'count') n.subField = e.target.value; else n.aggField = e.target.value; }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-op]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condOp); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.op = e.target.value; wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-subOp]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condSubop); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.subOp = e.target.value; wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-compareOp]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condCompareop); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.compareOp = e.target.value; }));
  ['value','subValue','compareValue'].forEach(attr => {
    root.querySelectorAll(`[data-cond-${attr}]`).forEach(el => {
      ['input','change'].forEach(evt => el.addEventListener(evt, e => {
        const key = 'cond' + attr.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + attr.slice(1);
        const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset[key]); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return;
        n[attr] = e.target.value;
      }));
    });
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-del]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condDel); if(!p) return; const g = getParentByPath(p); if(!g) return; g.parent.rules.splice(g.idx,1); wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-group-del]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', e => { const path = parsePath(e.target.dataset.groupDel); if(!path) return; const g = getParentByPath(path); if(!g) return; g.parent.rules.splice(g.idx,1); wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-group-logic]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const path = parsePath(e.target.dataset.groupLogic); const g = getNodeByPath(path); if(g){ g.logic = e.target.value; } }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-group-add-cond]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', e => { const path = parsePath(e.target.dataset.groupAddCond); const g = getNodeByPath(path); if(g && g.rules) g.rules.push({ field:'line.category', op:'eq', value:'مختبر' }); wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-group-add-group]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', e => { const path = parsePath(e.target.dataset.groupAddGroup); const g = getNodeByPath(path); if(g && g.rules) g.rules.push({ logic:'OR', rules:[{ field:'line.billedUnit', op:'gt', value:500 }] }); wizRenderConditions(); }));
  if(typeof bindReorderButtons === 'function') bindReorderButtons();
}

function createGhost(sourceEl, e){
  const clone = sourceEl.cloneNode(true);
  clone.classList.add('drag-ghost');
  clone.style.width = sourceEl.offsetWidth + 'px';
  const rect = sourceEl.getBoundingClientRect();
  clone.style.left = '0px';
  clone.style.top = '0px';
  clone.style.transform = `translate(${e.clientX+12}px, ${e.clientY+12}px)`;
  document.body.appendChild(clone);
  dragGhost = clone;
}

function updateGhost(e){
  if(!dragGhost) return;
  rafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    if(dragGhost) dragGhost.style.transform = `translate(${e.clientX+12}px, ${e.clientY+12}px)`;
  });
}

function showDropIndicator(targetEl, position){
  clearDropIndicators();
  if(position === 'before') targetEl.classList.add('drop-before');
  else if(position === 'after') targetEl.classList.add('drop-after');
  else if(position === 'into') targetEl.classList.add('drop-into');
  else if(position === 'before-group') targetEl.classList.add('drop-before-group');
  else if(position === 'after-group') targetEl.classList.add('drop-after-group');
}

function clearDropIndicators(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.drop-before,.drop-after,.drop-before-group,.drop-after-group,.drop-into,.drop-target-end,.drop-active').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.remove('drop-before','drop-after','drop-before-group','drop-after-group','drop-into','drop-target-end','drop-active');
  });
}

function recordFlipFirst(){
  flipFirst = {};
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-drag-path]').forEach(el => {
    flipFirst[el.dataset.dragPath] = el.getBoundingClientRect();
  });
}

function playFlip(){
  if(!flipFirst) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-drag-path]').forEach(el => {
    const first = flipFirst[el.dataset.dragPath];
    if(!first) return;
    const last = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    const dx = first.left - last.left;
    const dy = first.top - last.top;
    if(dx || dy){
      el.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px)`;
      el.style.transition = 'none';
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        el.style.transition = 'transform .2s ease';
        el.style.transform = '';
      });
    }
  });
  flipFirst = null;
}

function flashMovedItem(path){
  const el = document.querySelector(`[data-drag-path="${path}"]`);
  if(el){ el.classList.add('flash'); setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('flash'), 400); }
}

function autoScroll(e){
  const container = document.getElementById('condList');
  if(!container) return;
  const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
  const threshold = 40;
  if(e.clientY < rect.top + threshold){ container.scrollTop -= 8; }
  else if(e.clientY > rect.bottom - threshold){ container.scrollTop += 8; }
}

function clearDragState(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.dragging,.drop-before,.drop-after,.drop-before-group,.drop-after-group,.drop-into,.drop-target-end,.drop-active').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.remove('dragging','drop-before','drop-after','drop-before-group','drop-after-group','drop-into','drop-target-end','drop-active');
  });
  if(dragGhost){ dragGhost.remove(); dragGhost = null; }
  document.body.style.userSelect = '';
  if(rafId){ cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); rafId = null; }
  if(autoScrollInterval){ clearInterval(autoScrollInterval); autoScrollInterval = null; }
  dragSrcPath = null; dragSrcParent = null; dragSrcIndex = -1;
  dropTarget = null; flipFirst = null; dragThresholdMet = false;
}

function handleDragStart(e){
  const handle = e.target.closest('.cond-drag-handle');
  if(!handle) return;
  const item = handle.closest('[data-drag-path]');
  if(!item) return;
  dragSrcPath = item.dataset.dragPath;
  const info = getPathInfo(dragSrcPath);
  if(!info) return;
  dragSrcParent = info.parentPath;
  dragSrcIndex = info.index;
  dragStartX = e.clientX; dragStartY = e.clientY;
  dragThresholdMet = false;
  e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
  e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', dragSrcPath);
  item.classList.add('dragging');
  item.setAttribute('aria-grabbed', 'true');
  document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';
  createGhost(item, e);
  recordFlipFirst();
  // Activate drop zones
  document.querySelectorAll('.cond-list').forEach(cl => cl.classList.add('drop-active'));
}

function handleDrag(e){
  if(!dragSrcPath) return;
  if(!dragThresholdMet){
    const dx = e.clientX - dragStartX, dy = e.clientY - dragStartY;
    if(Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy) < 5) return;
    dragThresholdMet = true;
  }
  updateGhost(e);
  autoScroll(e);
}

function handleDragOver(e){
  if(!dragSrcPath) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
  const target = e.target.closest('[data-drag-path],[data-drop-container]');
  if(!target) return;
  const isGroup = target.classList.contains('cond-group');
  const isItem = target.classList.contains('cond-item');
  const isContainer = target.hasAttribute('data-drop-container') && !target.hasAttribute('data-drag-path');
  // Determine position
  let position = 'after';
  let targetPath = target.dataset.dragPath || '';
  let containerPath = target.dataset.dropContainer || '';
  if(isGroup && e.target.closest('.cond-group-head')){
    // Dropping into group
    position = 'into';
    targetPath = target.dataset.dragPath;
    containerPath = targetPath;
  } else if(isItem){
    const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    const midY = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
    position = e.clientY < midY ? 'before' : 'after';
  } else if(isContainer){
    position = 'end';
    containerPath = target.dataset.dropContainer || '';
  }
  // Validate: block self/descendant drop
  if(targetPath && (dragSrcPath === targetPath || isAncestor(dragSrcPath, targetPath))) {
    e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'none';
    clearDropIndicators();
    return;
  }
  // Update indicators
  clearDropIndicators();
  if(position === 'into'){
    target.classList.add('drop-into');
  } else if(position === 'before'){
    target.classList.add('drop-before');
  } else if(position === 'after'){
    target.classList.add('drop-after');
  } else if(position === 'end'){
    target.classList.add('drop-target-end');
  }
  dropTarget = { containerPath, targetPath, position };
}

function handleDragLeave(e){
  // small delay to avoid flicker
  setTimeout(() => {
    const t = e.target;
    if(t && t.classList) t.classList.remove('drop-before','drop-after','drop-into','drop-before-group','drop-after-group','drop-target-end');
  }, 50);
}

function handleDrop(e){
  if(!dragSrcPath || !dropTarget) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const { containerPath, targetPath, position } = dropTarget;
  // Validate
  if(dragSrcPath === targetPath) { clearDragState(); return; }
  if(isAncestor(dragSrcPath, targetPath)) { clearDragState(); return; }
  // Determine insertion index
  let insertIndex = 0;
  const targetArr = containerPath === '' ? wizState.conditions.rules : getPathInfo(containerPath)?.item?.rules;
  if(!targetArr){ clearDragState(); return; }
  if(position === 'into' || position === 'end'){
    insertIndex = targetArr.length;
  } else if(targetPath){
    const targetInfo = getPathInfo(targetPath);
    if(targetInfo){
      insertIndex = position === 'before' ? targetInfo.index : targetInfo.index + 1;
    }
  }
  // Adjust index if same container and src before target
  const srcInfo = getPathInfo(dragSrcPath);
  if(srcInfo && srcInfo.parentPath === containerPath && srcInfo.index < insertIndex){
    insertIndex--;
  }
  // Remove source
  const item = removeAtPath(dragSrcPath);
  if(!item){ clearDragState(); return; }
  // Insert at target
  insertAtPath(containerPath, insertIndex, item);
  // Re-render
  wizRenderConditions();
  // Flash moved item
  setTimeout(() => flashMovedItem(item.logic ? 'g' + wizState.conditions.rules.indexOf(item) : 'c' + wizState.conditions.rules.indexOf(item)), 50);
  // FLIP
  playFlip();
  clearDragState();
}

function handleDragEnd(e){
  clearDragState();
}

let dragSrcPath = null, dragSrcParent = null, dragSrcIndex = -1;
let dragGhost = null, dropTarget = null, flipFirst = null, rafId = null;
let dragThresholdMet = false, dragStartX = 0, dragStartY = 0;
let autoScrollInterval = null;
let undoStack = [];

function getPathInfo(path){
  if(!path) return null;
  const nums = String(path).match(/\d+/g);
  if(!nums) return null;
  const idxs = nums.map(Number);
  let parentArr = wizState.conditions.rules;
  let parentPath = '';
  for(let i=0;i<idxs.length-1;i++){
    const node = parentArr[idxs[i]];
    if(!node || !Array.isArray(node.rules)) return null;
    parentArr = node.rules;
    // rebuild parentPath
    if(i===0) parentPath = (node.logic?'g':'c')+idxs[i];
    else parentPath += '_' + (node.rules && node.rules[idxs[i+1]] && node.rules[idxs[i+1]].logic ? 'g' : 'c') + idxs[i+1];
  }
  const index = idxs[idxs.length-1];
  if(index < 0 || index >= parentArr.length) return null;
  return { parentArray: parentArr, index, item: parentArr[index], parentPath: idxs.length===1 ? '' : path.split('_').slice(0,-1).join('_') };
}

function getParentPath(path){
  if(!path) return '';
  const parts = String(path).split('_');
  return parts.length <= 1 ? '' : parts.slice(0,-1).join('_');
}

function isAncestor(srcPath, targetPath){
  if(!srcPath || !targetPath) return false;
  return String(targetPath).startsWith(String(srcPath)+'_');
}

function removeAtPath(path){
  const info = getPathInfo(path);
  if(!info) return null;
  const [removed] = info.parentArray.splice(info.index, 1);
  return removed;
}

function insertAtPath(containerPath, index, item){
  const targetArr = containerPath === '' ? wizState.conditions.rules : (() => {
    const info = getPathInfo(containerPath);
    return info ? info.item.rules : null;
  })();
  if(!targetArr) return false;
  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(index, targetArr.length));
  targetArr.splice(idx, 0, item);
  return true;
}

function moveCondition(path, direction){
  const info = getPathInfo(path);
  if(!info) return;
  const arr = info.parentArray;
  const idx = info.index;
  const parentPath = info.parentPath;
  if(direction === 'up'){
    if(idx > 0){
      [arr[idx-1], arr[idx]] = [arr[idx], arr[idx-1]];
    } else if(parentPath !== ''){
      // move out of group: insert before the group in parent
      const item = arr.splice(idx, 1)[0];
      const groupInfo = getPathInfo(parentPath);
      if(groupInfo){
        const groupParent = groupInfo.parentArray;
        const groupIdx = groupInfo.index;
        groupParent.splice(groupIdx, 0, item);
      }
    }
  } else {
    if(idx < arr.length - 1){
      [arr[idx], arr[idx+1]] = [arr[idx+1], arr[idx]];
    } else if(parentPath !== ''){
      const item = arr.splice(idx, 1)[0];
      const groupInfo = getPathInfo(parentPath);
      if(groupInfo){
        const groupParent = groupInfo.parentArray;
        const groupIdx = groupInfo.index;
        groupParent.splice(groupIdx + 1, 0, item);
      }
    }
  }
  wizRenderConditions();
  setTimeout(() => flashMovedItem(path), 50);
}

function initDragDrop(){
  const root = document.getElementById('condList');
  if(!root || root.dataset.dndInit === '1') return;
  root.dataset.dndInit = '1';
  root.addEventListener('dragstart', handleDragStart);
  root.addEventListener('drag', handleDrag);
  root.addEventListener('dragover', handleDragOver);
  root.addEventListener('dragleave', handleDragLeave);
  root.addEventListener('drop', handleDrop);
  root.addEventListener('dragend', handleDragEnd);
  // window-level cleanup
  window.addEventListener('blur', clearDragState);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if(document.hidden) clearDragState(); });
  // keyboard
  root.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if(!e.altKey) return;
    const handle = e.target.closest('.cond-drag-handle');
    if(!handle) return;
    const item = handle.closest('[data-drag-path]');
    if(!item) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if(e.key === 'ArrowUp') moveCondition(item.dataset.dragPath, 'up');
    else if(e.key === 'ArrowDown') moveCondition(item.dataset.dragPath, 'down');
  });
}
// Bind reorder buttons (called from wizBindConditionHandlers or after render)

function bindReorderButtons(){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-move-up]').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); moveCondition(btn.dataset.moveUp, 'up'); });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-move-down]').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); moveCondition(btn.dataset.moveDown, 'down'); });
  });
}

function wizRenderTiers(){

  box.innerHTML = wizState.tiers.map((t,i) => `<div class="tier-row" style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr auto;gap:6px;margin-bottom:6px">

    <input type="number" placeholder="إلى" value="${t.to||''}" data-tier-idx="${i}" data-tier-field="to">
    <input type="number" placeholder="%" value="${t.rate||0}" data-tier-idx="${i}" data-tier-field="rate">
    <button type="button" class="btn-xs danger" data-tier-del="${i}">×</button>
  </div>`).join('');
  box.querySelectorAll('input[data-tier-idx]').forEach(inp => inp.addEventListener('change', e => {
    const i = Number(e.target.dataset.tierIdx); const f = e.target.dataset.tierField;

  }));

}

function wizSyncInputs(){
  if(wizState.step === 2){
    wizState.name = $('#wizName').value.trim(); wizState.notes = $('#wizNotes').value.trim();
    if(wizState.type !== 'composite') wizState.rate = Number($('#wizRate').value)||0;
    if(wizState.type === 'custom') wizState.action = $('#wizAction').value;
    else if(wizState.type === 'coverage_cap') wizState.action = 'apply_cap';
    else if(wizState.type === 'retiree_copay') wizState.action = 'apply_copay';
    else if(wizState.type === 'composite') wizState.action = 'composite';
    if(wizState.type === 'composite'){ wizState.compositeConfig.applyMode = $('#wizCompositeMode').value; wizState.compositeConfig.requireAllMatch = $('#wizRequireAll').checked; }
    const te = $('#wizTiersEnable'); if(te) wizState.tiersEnabled = te.checked;
    wizState.requires.mode = $('#wizRequiresMode').value;
  }
  if(wizState.step === 3){ wizState.from = $('#wizFrom').value; wizState.to = $('#wizTo').value; }
  if(wizState.step === 4) wizState.conditions.logic = $('#condLogic').value;
}
function wizValidate(step){
  if(step === 1){ if(!wizState.type){ showWarning('تنبيه','اختر'); return false; } return true; }
  if(step === 2){
    if(!wizState.name.trim()){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الاسم'); return false; }
    if(wizState.type === 'composite'){ if(!wizState.compositeConfig.childRuleIds.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','اختر'); return false; } }
    else if(!wizState.rate || wizState.rate <= 0){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل النسبة'); return false; }
    return true;
  }
  if(step === 3){
    if(!wizState.from){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل السريان'); return false; }
    if(wizState.to && wizState.to < wizState.from){ showWarning('تنبيه','النهاية بعد'); return false; }
    return true;
  }
  return true;
}
function wizGoTo(step){
  if(step < wizState.step){ wizSyncInputs(); wizState.step = step; wizRenderStep(); return; }
  for(let s = wizState.step; s < step; s++){ wizSyncInputs(); if(!wizValidate(s)) return; }
  wizState.step = step; wizRenderStep();
}
function wizNext(){ wizSyncInputs(); if(!wizValidate(wizState.step)) return; if(wizState.step === 6){ wizSave(); return; } wizState.step++; wizRenderStep(); }
function wizBack(){ wizSyncInputs(); if(wizState.step > 1){ wizState.step--; wizRenderStep(); } }
function wizSave(){
  wizSyncInputs();
  if(!wizValidate(5)) return;
  const y = new Date(wizState.from).getFullYear(), pre = 'DR-'+y+'-';
  let m = 0;
  deductionRules.forEach(x => { if(x.id.startsWith(pre)){ const n = parseInt(x.id.split('-').pop(), 10); if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m,n); } });
  const rule = { id:pre+String(m+1).padStart(3,'0'), name:wizState.name, type:wizState.type, action:wizState.action, rate:wizState.type === 'composite' ? 0 : wizState.rate, isComposite:wizState.type === 'composite', compositeConfig:wizState.type === 'composite' ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(wizState.compositeConfig)) : null, requires:(wizState.requires.ruleIds && wizState.requires.ruleIds.length) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(wizState.requires)) : null, scope:{ providerIds:[...wizState.providerIds], contractIds:[...wizState.contractIds], claimTypes:[...wizState.ctypes] }, conditions:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(wizState.conditions)), effectiveFrom:wizState.from, effectiveTo:wizState.to||'', active:true, enabled:true, notes:wizState.notes, tiers: (wizState.tiersEnabled && wizState.tiers && wizState.tiers.length) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(wizState.tiers)) : null, tiersEnabled: !!wizState.tiersEnabled };
  deductionRules.push(rule); closeDrWizard(); renderDeductionRules(); showSuccess('تم', rule.id);
}

let servicesSearchTerm = '', servicesFilter = 'all', servicesSort = 'name', servicesSortDir = 'asc';
let servicesAdv = { code:'', name:'', category:'', tariff:'', min:'', max:'' };
let servicesAdvOpen = false;
let editingServiceForm = {tariffId:null,index:null};
let servicesTariffFilter = 'all';

function getAllServicesFlat(){
  const out = [];
  (typeof tariffs !== 'undefined' ? tariffs : []).forEach(t => {
    (t.services||[]).forEach((s, idx) => { out.push({ code:s.code, name:s.name, category:s.category, tariff:t.name, tariffId:t.id, index:idx, base:s.basePrice, current:s.currentPrice, covered:!!s.covered }); });
  });
  return out;
}

function renderServicesTariffFilter(){
  const sel = $('#servicesTariffFilter'); if(!sel) return;
  const cur = sel.value || 'all';
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="all">كل اللوائح</option>' + (tariffs||[]).map(t => `<option value="${escapeHtml(t.id)}">${escapeHtml(t.name)}</option>`).join('');
  sel.value = (tariffs||[]).some(t=>t.id===cur) ? cur : 'all';
}

function renderServices(){
  renderServicesTariffFilter();
  let list = getAllServicesFlat();
  if(servicesTariffFilter !== 'all') list = list.filter(s => s.tariffId === servicesTariffFilter);
  if(servicesSearchTerm){
    const q = servicesSearchTerm.toLowerCase();
    list = list.filter(s => (s.code||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (s.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (s.category||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (s.tariff||'').toLowerCase().includes(q));
  }
  if(servicesFilter === 'covered') list = list.filter(s => s.covered);
  if(servicesFilter === 'uncovered') list = list.filter(s => !s.covered);
  if(servicesAdv.code){ const q=servicesAdv.code.toLowerCase(); list=list.filter(s=>(s.code||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
  if(servicesAdv.name){ const q=servicesAdv.name.toLowerCase(); list=list.filter(s=>(s.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
  if(servicesAdv.category){ const q=servicesAdv.category.toLowerCase(); list=list.filter(s=>(s.category||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
  if(servicesAdv.tariff){ const q=servicesAdv.tariff.toLowerCase(); list=list.filter(s=>(s.tariff||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
  if(servicesAdv.min){ const n=Number(servicesAdv.min); list=list.filter(s=>(s.base||0)>=n); }
  if(servicesAdv.max){ const n=Number(servicesAdv.max); list=list.filter(s=>(s.base||0)<=n); }
  const dir = servicesSortDir === 'desc' ? -1 : 1;
  list.sort((a,b) => {
    let va, vb;
    switch(servicesSort){
      case 'code': va=a.code||''; vb=b.code||''; break;
      case 'base': va=a.base||0; vb=b.base||0; break;
      case 'current': va=a.current||0; vb=b.current||0; break;
      case 'tariff': va=a.tariff||''; vb=b.tariff||''; break;
      case 'category': va=a.category||''; vb=b.category||''; break;
      default: va=a.name||''; vb=b.name||'';
    }
    if(typeof va === 'string') return va.localeCompare(vb)*dir;
    return (va-vb)*dir;
  });

  const body = $('#servicesBody'); if(!body) return;
  if(!list.length){ body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:36px;color:var(--muted)">لا خدمات</td></tr>'; }
  else body.innerHTML = list.map(s => `<tr>
    <td class="mono">${escapeHtml(s.code)}</td>
    <td>${escapeHtml(s.name)}</td>
    <td>${escapeHtml(s.category||'—')}</td>
    <td>${escapeHtml(s.tariff)}</td>
    <td class="mono">${fmt(s.base||0)}</td>
    <td class="mono">${fmt(s.current||0)}</td>
    <td>${s.covered?'<span class="badge ok sm">مغطاة</span>':'<span class="badge bad sm">غير مغطاة</span>'}</td>
    <td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:3px"><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-service="${s.tariffId}|${s.index}">تعديل</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-del-service="${s.tariffId}|${s.index}">حذف</button></div></td>
  </tr>`).join('');
  const kpi = $('#servicesKpi');
  if(kpi){ kpi.innerHTML = `<div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">الخدمات</div><div class="kpi-val">${list.length} <small>خدمة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('shield')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">من أصل ${getAllServicesFlat().length}</div></div>`; }
}

function showServiceFormPanel(tariffId, idx){
  if(typeof tariffs === 'undefined') return;
  if(!tariffs.length){
    const t = { id: nextTariffNumber(daysFromNow(0)), name:'خدمات عامة', type:'general', status:'active', effectiveDate:daysFromNow(0), expiryDate:'', basePriceLevel:100, services:[] };
    tariffs.push(t);
  }
  const sel = $('#servicesTariffFilter');
  const tid = tariffId || ((sel && sel.value !== 'all') ? sel.value : tariffs[0].id);
  const t = tariffs.find(x=>x.id===tid) || tariffs[0];
  // populate tariff select
  const ts = $('#sfTariff'); if(ts){ ts.innerHTML = tariffs.map(x=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(x.id)}">${escapeHtml(x.name)}</option>`).join(''); ts.value = t.id; }
  // reset or load
  const svc = (idx !== null && idx !== undefined && t.services && t.services[idx]) ? t.services[idx] : null;
  editingServiceForm = { tariffId: t.id, index: (svc? idx : null) };
  $('#sfCode').value = svc? svc.code : '';
  $('#sfCategory').value = svc? svc.category : 'استشارة';
  $('#sfName').value = svc? svc.name : '';
  $('#sfBasePrice').value = svc? svc.basePrice : '';
  $('#sfCurrentPrice').value = svc? svc.currentPrice : '';
  $('#sfCovered').checked = svc? !!svc.covered : true;
  $('#sfRequiresPriorApproval').checked = svc? !!svc.requiresPriorApproval : false;
  const panel = $('#serviceFormPanel'); if(panel) panel.style.display = 'block';
}
function hideServiceFormPanel(){ const panel = $('#serviceFormPanel'); if(panel) panel.style.display = 'none'; editingServiceForm = {tariffId:null,index:null}; }
function saveServiceForm(){
  const tid = $('#sfTariff').value;
  const t = tariffs.find(x=>x.id===tid); if(!t){ showWarning('تنبيه','اختر اللائحة'); return; }
  if(!t.services) t.services = [];
  const code = $('#sfCode').value.trim(), name = $('#sfName').value.trim(), cat = $('#sfCategory').value;
  const bp = Number($('#sfBasePrice').value)||0, cp = Number($('#sfCurrentPrice').value)||0;
  if(!code){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الكود'); return; }
  if(!name){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الاسم'); return; }
  if(bp <= 0){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل سعراً'); return; }
  const data = { code, name, category:cat, basePrice:bp, currentPrice:cp||bp, covered:$('#sfCovered').checked, requiresPriorApproval:$('#sfRequiresPriorApproval').checked };
  const idx = editingServiceForm.index;
  if(idx !== null && t.services[idx]){
    // if tariff changed, move? keep simple: update in original tariff
    const origT = tariffs.find(x=>x.id===editingServiceForm.tariffId);
    if(origT && origT !== t){
      // remove from original
      if(origT.services) origT.services.splice(idx,1);
    }
    // update target
    const existingIdx = t.services.findIndex(s=>s.code===code && t.services.indexOf(s)!==idx);
    // just replace if same tariff
    if(editingServiceForm.tariffId === tid && t.services[idx]) { t.services[idx] = data; }
    else { if(t.services.some(s=>s.code===code)){ showWarning('تنبيه','الكود مستخدم'); return; } t.services.push(data); }
    showSuccess('تم','تم');
  } else {
    if(t.services.some(s=>s.code===code)){ showWarning('تنبيه','الكود مستخدم'); return; }
    t.services.push(data); showSuccess('تم','تم');
  }
  if(typeof persistData === 'function') persistData(true);
  hideServiceFormPanel(); renderServices(); renderTariffs();
}

function editServiceAt(tid, idx){
  const t = tariffs.find(x=>x.id===tid); if(!t || !t.services) return;
  if(typeof openServiceModal === 'function'){ openServiceModal(idx, t.id); }
}

function deleteServiceAt(tid, idx){
  const t = tariffs.find(x=>x.id===tid); if(!t || !t.services || !t.services[idx]) return;
  const s = t.services[idx];
  showDelete('حذف الخدمة', `سيتم حذف "${s.name}".`, () => {
    t.services.splice(idx,1);
    if(typeof persistData === 'function') persistData(true);
    renderServices();
    showSuccess('تم','تم الحذف');
  });
}

function importServicesCSV(text){
  const lines = String(text||'').trim().split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean);
  if(!lines.length) return;
  const header = lines[0].split(',').map(h=>h.trim().toLowerCase());
  const idx = (names) => { for(const n of names){ const i = header.indexOf(n); if(i>=0) return i; } return -1; };
  const iCode = idx(['code','كود']);
  const iName = idx(['name','اسم','service','خدمة']);
  const iCat = idx(['category','فئة']);
  const iBase = idx(['base','أساسي','baseprice']);
  const iCur = idx(['current','حالي','currentprice']);
  const iCov = idx(['covered','تغطية']);
  const iTariff = idx(['tariff','لائحة']);
  let count = 0;
  for(let i=1;i<lines.length;i++){
    const cols = lines[i].split(',');
    const get = (j) => (j>=0 ? (cols[j]||'').trim() : '');
    const name = get(iName);
    if(!name) continue;
    const tariffName = get(iTariff);
    let tariff = tariffs.find(t => t.name === tariffName) || tariffs[0];
    if(!tariff){
      tariff = { id: nextTariffNumber(daysFromNow(0)), name: tariffName || 'خدمات مستوردة', type:'general', status:'active', effectiveDate:daysFromNow(0), expiryDate:'', basePriceLevel:100, services:[] };
      tariffs.push(tariff);
    }
    if(!tariff.services) tariff.services = [];
    const code = get(iCode) || ('SVC-'+(tariff.services.length+1));
    const category = get(iCat) || 'أخرى';
    const base = Number(get(iBase))||0;
    const current = Number(get(iCur))||base;
    const covRaw = get(iCov).toLowerCase();
    const covered = !(covRaw === 'false' || covRaw === '0' || covRaw === 'غير مغطاة' || covRaw === 'no');
    tariff.services.push({ code, name, category, basePrice:base, currentPrice:current, covered, requiresPriorApproval:false });
    count++;
  }
  if(typeof persistData === 'function') persistData(true);
  renderServices();
  if(typeof renderTariffs === 'function') renderTariffs();
  showSuccess('تم', `تم استيراد ${count} خدمة`);
}

function bindServicesControls(){
  const s = $('#servicesSearch'); if(s && !s.dataset.bound){ s.dataset.bound='1'; s.addEventListener('input', e => { servicesSearchTerm = e.target.value.trim(); renderServices(); }); }
  const tf = $('#servicesTariffFilter'); if(tf && !tf.dataset.bound){ tf.dataset.bound='1'; tf.addEventListener('change', e => { servicesTariffFilter = e.target.value; renderServices(); }); }
  const sort = $('#servicesSort'); if(sort && !sort.dataset.bound){ sort.dataset.bound='1'; sort.addEventListener('change', e => { servicesSort = e.target.value; renderServices(); }); }
  const dir = $('#servicesSortDir'); if(dir && !dir.dataset.bound){ dir.dataset.bound='1'; dir.addEventListener('change', e => { servicesSortDir = e.target.value; renderServices(); }); }
  const chips = $('#servicesFilterChips'); if(chips && !chips.dataset.bound){ chips.dataset.bound='1'; chips.addEventListener('click', e => { const c = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!c) return; $$('#servicesFilterChips .chip').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active')); c.classList.add('active'); servicesFilter = c.dataset.sfilter; renderServices(); }); }
  const toggle = $('#servicesAdvToggle'); if(toggle && !toggle.dataset.bound){ toggle.dataset.bound='1'; toggle.addEventListener('click', () => { servicesAdvOpen = !servicesAdvOpen; const p = $('#servicesAdvPanel'); if(p) p.style.display = servicesAdvOpen ? 'block' : 'none'; }); }
  const apply = $('#servicesAdvApply'); if(apply && !apply.dataset.bound){ apply.dataset.bound='1'; apply.addEventListener('click', () => { servicesAdv.code = $('#advCode').value.trim(); servicesAdv.name = $('#advName').value.trim(); servicesAdv.category = $('#advCategory').value.trim(); servicesAdv.tariff = $('#advTariff').value.trim(); servicesAdv.min = $('#advMinPrice').value.trim(); servicesAdv.max = $('#advMaxPrice').value.trim(); renderServices(); }); }
  const reset = $('#servicesAdvReset'); if(reset && !reset.dataset.bound){ reset.dataset.bound='1'; reset.addEventListener('click', () => { servicesAdv = {code:'',name:'',category:'',tariff:'',min:'',max:''}; ['advCode','advName','advCategory','advTariff','advMinPrice','advMaxPrice'].forEach(id=>{const el=$('#'+id); if(el) el.value='';}); renderServices(); }); }
  const impBtn = $('#servicesImportBtn'); const impFile = $('#servicesImportFile');
  if(impBtn && impFile && !impBtn.dataset.bound){ impBtn.dataset.bound='1'; impBtn.addEventListener('click', () => impFile.click()); impFile.addEventListener('change', e => { const f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0]; if(f){ const r = new FileReader(); r.onload = () => importServicesCSV(String(r.result||'')); r.readAsText(f,'utf-8'); } e.target.value=''; }); }
  const addBtn = $('#servicesAddBtn'); if(addBtn && !addBtn.dataset.bound){ addBtn.dataset.bound='1'; addBtn.addEventListener('click', () => showServiceFormPanel()); }
  const sfSave = $('#sfSaveBtn'); if(sfSave && !sfSave.dataset.bound){ sfSave.dataset.bound='1'; sfSave.addEventListener('click', () => saveServiceForm()); }
  const sfCancel = $('#sfCancelBtn'); if(sfCancel && !sfCancel.dataset.bound){ sfCancel.dataset.bound='1'; sfCancel.addEventListener('click', () => hideServiceFormPanel()); }
  const body = $('#servicesBody'); if(body && !body.dataset.bound){ body.dataset.bound='1'; body.addEventListener('click', e => {
    const ed = e.target.closest('[data-edit-service]'); if(ed){ const [tid, idx] = ed.dataset.editService.split('|'); showServiceFormPanel(tid, Number(idx)); return; }
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-del-service]'); if(del){ const [tid, idx] = del.dataset.delService.split('|'); deleteServiceAt(tid, Number(idx)); return; }
  }); }
}

function bindSessionsControls(){
  const s = document.getElementById('sessionsSearch');
  const c = document.getElementById('sessionsFilterChips');
  const n = document.getElementById('newSessionBtn');
  const l = document.getElementById('sessionsList');
  const sw = document.getElementById('rvSessionSwitcher');
  if(s){ let t; s.addEventListener('input', e => { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(() => { sessionsSearch = e.target.value.trim(); renderSessions(); }, 180); }); }
  if(c){ c.addEventListener('click', e => { const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return; $$('#sessionsFilterChips .chip').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active')); chip.classList.add('active'); sessionsFilter = chip.dataset.sfilter; renderSessions(); }); }
  if(n){ n.addEventListener('click', () => { navHistory = []; navigateTo('claims', {noPush:true}); showToast('اختر مطالبة واضغط «مراجعة»'); }); }
  if(l){
    l.addEventListener('click', e => {
      const r = e.target.closest('[data-resume-session]');
      if(r){ const ss = workSessions.find(x => x.id === r.dataset.resumeSession); if(ss){ const info = getSessionClaimInfo(ss); if(info.stale){ deleteSessionWithDialog(ss.id); return; } if(activeSessionId && activeSessionId !== ss.id){ upsertCurrentSession(); activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = []; } resumeSession(ss.id); } return; }
      const d = e.target.closest('[data-delete-session]'); if(d) deleteSessionWithDialog(d.dataset.deleteSession);
    });
  }
  if(sw){ sw.addEventListener('change', e => { const sid = e.target.value; if(!sid) return; upsertCurrentSession(); activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = []; $('#reviewModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); rvActiveInvoiceIdx = 0; rvActivePatientIdx = 0; activeSessionId = null; resumeSession(sid); }); }
}
function deleteSessionWithDialog(id){
  const s = workSessions.find(x => x.id === id); if(!s) return;
  const info = getSessionClaimInfo(s);
  showDelete('حذف الجلسة', `"${s.claimId} — ${info.patient}".`, () => {
    workSessions = workSessions.filter(x => x.id !== id);
    if(activeSessionId === id){ activeSessionId = null; $('#reviewModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = []; }
    persistSessions(); updateSessionsBadge(); renderSessions(); showSuccess('تم','تم');
  });
}
// loadSessions() is called from initApp()

let workSessions = [];
let activeSessionId = null;
let sessionsFilter = 'all';
let sessionsSearch = '';
function genSessionId(){ return 'SES-'+Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*1000); }
function loadSessions(){
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SESSIONS_KEY);
    if(raw){ const p = JSON.parse(raw); if(Array.isArray(p)) workSessions = p.map(s => ({ ...s, status:'paused' })); }
  } catch(e){}
}
function persistSessions(){ try { localStorage.setItem(SESSIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(workSessions)); } catch(e){} }
function findSessionByClaim(claimId){ return workSessions.find(s => s.claimId === claimId); }
function getSessionClaimInfo(s){
  const live = getClaimById(s.claimId);
  const snap = s.claimSnapshot || {};
  return { id:s.claimId, patient:(live&&live.patient)||snap.patient||'—', provider:(live&&live.provider)||snap.provider||'—', billed:(live&&live.billed)||snap.billed||0, approved:(live&&live.approved)||snap.approved||0, stale:!live };
}
function captureCurrentSessionState(){
  if(!activeClaim) return null;
  const ex = findSessionByClaim(activeClaim.id);
  return {
    id:(ex&&ex.id)||activeSessionId||genSessionId(),
    claimId:activeClaim.id,
    claimSnapshot:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(activeClaim)),
    workingInvoices:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(workingInvoices||[])),
    rvActiveInvoiceIdx:rvActiveInvoiceIdx||0,
    rvActivePatientIdx:rvActivePatientIdx||0,
    reviewFormState:{ reason:($('#rvReason')&&$('#rvReason').value)||'', notes:($('#rvNotes')&&$('#rvNotes').value)||'', paStatus:($('#paStatus')&&$('#paStatus').value)||'not-requested', paRefNumber:($('#paRefNumber')&&$('#paRefNumber').value)||'', paDate:($('#paDate')&&$('#paDate').value)||'', claimType:activeClaim.type||'employee' },
    createdAt:(ex&&ex.createdAt)||new Date().toISOString(),
    lastActiveAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    status:'paused',
    name:(ex&&ex.name)||null
  };
}
function upsertCurrentSession(){
  const s = captureCurrentSessionState(); if(!s) return null;
  const i = workSessions.findIndex(x => x.id === s.id);
  if(i >= 0) workSessions[i] = s; else workSessions.push(s);
  persistSessions(); updateSessionsBadge(); return s;
}
function updateSessionsBadge(){
  const b = document.getElementById('sessionsCount');
  if(!b) return;
  const n = workSessions.length;
  if(n > 0){ b.textContent = n; b.style.display = 'grid'; } else b.style.display = 'none';
  renderSessionSwitcher();
}
function renderSessionSwitcher(){
  const sel = document.getElementById('rvSessionSwitcher'); if(!sel) return;
  const others = workSessions.filter(s => s.id !== activeSessionId);
  if(!others.length){ sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">لا جلسات أخرى</option>'; sel.disabled = true; return; }
  sel.disabled = false;
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">⇄ تبديل...</option>' + others.map(s => { const info = getSessionClaimInfo(s); return `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}">${escapeHtml(s.claimId)} — ${escapeHtml(info.patient.substring(0,22))}</option>`; }).join('');
}
function resumeSession(sessionId){
  const s = workSessions.find(x => x.id === sessionId); if(!s){ showWarning('خطأ','غير موجودة'); return; }
  let live = getClaimById(s.claimId);
  if(!live){
    live = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s.claimSnapshot || {}));
    if(!live.id || !live.patient){ showWarning('خطأ','لا يمكن'); return; }
    claims.push(live); showToast(`تم استرداد ${live.id}`);
  }
  activeClaim = live;
  if(s.claimSnapshot && Array.isArray(s.claimSnapshot.invoices)) activeClaim.invoices = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s.claimSnapshot.invoices));
  workingInvoices = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s.workingInvoices || []));
  rvActiveInvoiceIdx = s.rvActiveInvoiceIdx || 0;
  rvActivePatientIdx = s.rvActivePatientIdx || 0;
  activeSessionId = s.id;
  s.status = 'active'; s.lastActiveAt = new Date().toISOString(); persistSessions();
  $('#rvSub').textContent = `${activeClaim.id} • ${activeClaim.patient}`;
  $('#rvId').value = activeClaim.id; $('#rvDate').value = activeClaim.date||'';
  $('#rvReceivedNumber').value = activeClaim.receivedNumber||'—'; $('#rvReceivedDate').value = activeClaim.receivedDate||'—';
  $('#rvPatient').value = activeClaim.patient||''; $('#rvProvider').value = activeClaim.provider||'';
  $('#rvReason').value = s.reviewFormState.reason||''; $('#rvNotes').value = s.reviewFormState.notes||'';
  if(s.reviewFormState.claimType) activeClaim.type = s.reviewFormState.claimType;
  $$('.rv-type-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.claimType === activeClaim.type));
  if(activeClaim.priorApproval && activeClaim.priorApproval.required){
    (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'block'; })($('#priorApprovalBox'));
    $('#paStatus').value = s.reviewFormState.paStatus || activeClaim.priorApproval.status || 'not-requested';
    $('#paRefNumber').value = s.reviewFormState.paRefNumber || activeClaim.priorApproval.refNumber || '';
    $('#paDate').value = s.reviewFormState.paDate || activeClaim.priorApproval.date || '';
    updatePAWarning();
  } else (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; })($('#priorApprovalBox'));
  renderReviewInvoices();
  clearSessionDirty();
  $('#reviewModal').classList.add('open'); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
  updateSessionsBadge(); renderSessions();
}
function formatTimeAgo(iso){
  if(!iso) return '—';
  const diff = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime())/1000);
  if(diff < 30) return 'الآن';
  if(diff < 3600) return `قبل ${Math.floor(diff/60)} د`;
  if(diff < 86400) return `قبل ${Math.floor(diff/3600)} س`;
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('ar-SA');
}
function renderSessionCard(s){
  const info = getSessionClaimInfo(s);
  const inv = (s.workingInvoices||[]).length;
  const pat = (s.workingInvoices||[]).reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.patients||[]).length,0);
  const lines = (s.workingInvoices||[]).reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.patients||[]).reduce((b,p)=>b+(p.lines||[]).length,0),0);
  const billed = (s.workingInvoices||[]).reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.patients||[]).reduce((b,p)=>b+(p.lines||[]).reduce((c,l)=>c+(l.qty*l.billedUnit),0),0),0);
  const appr = (s.workingInvoices||[]).reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.patients||[]).reduce((b,p)=>b+(p.lines||[]).reduce((c,l)=>c+(Number(l.approved)||0),0),0),0);
  const rate = billed ? ((billed-appr)/billed*100) : 0;
  let pc = 'paused', pt = 'موقوفة';
  if(info.stale){ pc = 'stale'; pt = 'مفقودة'; }
  else if(s.status === 'active'){ pc = 'active'; pt = 'نشطة'; }
  return `<div class="session-card ${s.id===activeSessionId?'active-session':''} ${info.stale?'stale':''}">
    <div class="sc-head"><div class="sc-claim"><div class="sc-claim-id">${escapeHtml(s.claimId)}</div><div class="sc-patient">${escapeHtml(info.patient)}</div><div class="sc-provider">${escapeHtml(info.provider)}</div></div><span class="sc-status-pill ${pc}">${pt}</span></div>
    <div class="sc-stats"><div><span>فواتير</span><b>${inv}</b></div><div><span>مرضى</span><b>${pat}</b></div><div><span>بنود</span><b>${lines}</b></div></div>
    <div class="sc-money"><span>مطالب: <b>${fmt(billed)}</b></span><span style="color:var(--ok)">معتمد: <b>${fmt(appr)}</b></span><span style="color:var(--bad)">استقطاع: <b>${rate.toFixed(1)}%</b></span></div>
    <div class="sc-foot"><div class="sc-time">🕐 ${formatTimeAgo(s.lastActiveAt)}</div><div class="sc-actions"><button class="btn-xs" data-resume-session="${s.id}">${info.stale?'⚠ حذف':'استئناف'}</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-delete-session="${s.id}">×</button></div></div>
  </div>`;
}
function renderSessions(){
  const box = document.getElementById('sessionsList'); if(!box) return;
  const activeN = workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='active').length;
  const pausedN = workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='paused').length;
  const kpi = document.getElementById('sessionsKpi');
  if(kpi){
    kpi.innerHTML = `
      <div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">الجلسات</div><div class="kpi-val">${workSessions.length} <small>جلسة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('file')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${activeN} نشطة · ${pausedN} موقوفة</div></div>
      <div class="kpi k2"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">قيد العمل</div><div class="kpi-val">${activeN} <small>جلسة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('shield')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${pausedN} بانتظار</div></div>
      <div class="kpi k3"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">مطالبات</div><div class="kpi-val">${workSessions.length} <small>مطالبة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('money')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">غير مكتملة</div></div>
      <div class="kpi k4"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">آخر نشاط</div><div class="kpi-val" style="font-size:13px">${workSessions.length?formatTimeAgo(workSessions.reduce((a,s)=> (s.lastActiveAt>a?s.lastActiveAt:a), workSessions[0].lastActiveAt)):'—'}</div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('check')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">تحديث تلقائي</div></div>`;
  }
  const filtered = workSessions.filter(s => {
    if(sessionsFilter === 'active' && s.status !== 'active') return false;
    if(sessionsFilter === 'paused' && s.status !== 'paused') return false;
    if(sessionsSearch){ const info = getSessionClaimInfo(s), q = sessionsSearch.toLowerCase(); return s.claimId.toLowerCase().includes(q) || info.patient.toLowerCase().includes(q) || info.provider.toLowerCase().includes(q); }
    return true;
  }).sort((a,b) => new Date(b.lastActiveAt) - new Date(a.lastActiveAt));
  if(!filtered.length){
    box.innerHTML = `<div class="session-empty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="2.5" y="5" width="19" height="14" rx="2.5"/><path d="M6.5 3v4M17.5 3v4M2.5 10h19"/></svg><p>${workSessions.length?'لا نتائج':'لا جلسات'}</p><span>${workSessions.length?'جرّب آخر':'ابدأ بمراجعة'}</span></div>`;
  } else box.innerHTML = `<div class="sessions-grid">${filtered.map(renderSessionCard).join('')}</div>`;
  updateSessionsBadge();
}

FILE: /home/administrator/TPA/js/shortcuts.js

function bindShortcuts(){
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
    if(ctrl && e.key === 'k'){ e.preventDefault(); navHistory=[]; navigateTo('search',{noPush:true}); const inp = document.getElementById('srchQuery')||document.getElementById('searchInput'); if(inp) setTimeout(()=>inp.focus(),50); return; }
    if(ctrl && e.key === 'n'){ e.preventDefault(); if(currentPage!=='intake') navigateTo('intake',{noPush:true}); setTimeout(()=>{ const b=document.getElementById('addBatchBtn'); if(b) b.click(); },50); return; }
    if(e.key === 'Escape'){
      const openModal = document.querySelector('.modal.open, .dialog-overlay.open');
      if(openModal){
        if(openModal.id === 'unifiedDialog'){ const cb = dialogState.onCancel; closeDialog(); if(cb) cb(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'reviewModal'){ closeReview(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'batchModal'){ closeBatchModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'providerModal'){ closeProviderModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'contractModal'){ closeContractModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'tariffModal'){ closeTariffModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'invoiceModal'){ closeInvoiceEditor(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'patientModal'){ closePatientEditor(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'lineModal'){ closeLineEditor(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'serviceModal'){ closeServiceModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'drWizardModal'){ closeDrWizard(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'drModal'){ closeDrModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'paProcModal'){ $('#paProcModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); }
        else { openModal.classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); }
        return;
      }
      if(window.innerWidth <= 900) closeSidebar();
      return;
    }
    if(e.key === '?' && !ctrl){ const a=document.activeElement; if(a && (a.tagName==='INPUT'||a.tagName==='TEXTAREA'||a.tagName==='SELECT')) return; e.preventDefault(); navHistory=[]; navigateTo('help',{noPush:true}); return; }
  });
}

FILE: /home/administrator/TPA/js/storage.js

let _persistTimer = null;
function persistData(immediate){
  if(_persistTimer) clearTimeout(_persistTimer);
  const doIt = () => {
    try {
      const payload = { version: DATA_VERSION, savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        data: { providers, contracts, tariffs, batches, claims, trash, deductionRules, paProcedures } };
      localStorage.setItem(DATA_STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
    } catch(e){ console.error('persistData failed', e); }
  };
  if(immediate) doIt();
  else _persistTimer = setTimeout(doIt, 250);
}
function loadPersistedData(){
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(DATA_STORE_KEY);
    if(!raw) return false;
    const p = JSON.parse(raw);
    if(!p || !p.data) return false;
    const d = p.data;
    if(Array.isArray(d.providers)) providers = d.providers;
    if(Array.isArray(d.contracts)) contracts = d.contracts;
    if(Array.isArray(d.tariffs)) tariffs = d.tariffs;
    if(Array.isArray(d.batches)) batches = d.batches;
    if(Array.isArray(d.claims)) claims = d.claims;
    if(Array.isArray(d.trash)) trash = d.trash;
    if(Array.isArray(d.deductionRules)) deductionRules = d.deductionRules;
    if(Array.isArray(d.paProcedures)) paProcedures = d.paProcedures;
    claims.forEach(c => {
      if(!c.invoices) c.invoices = [];
      if(!c.priorApproval) c.priorApproval = {required:false,status:'not-requested',refNumber:'',date:''};
    });
    return true;
  } catch(e){ console.error('loadPersistedData failed', e); return false; }
}
function trackBackup(){ try { localStorage.setItem(LAST_BACKUP_KEY, String(Date.now())); } catch(e){} }
function getLastBackupAgo(){
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LAST_BACKUP_KEY);
    if(!raw) return null;
    const ms = Date.now() - parseInt(raw, 10);
    if(ms < 60000) return 'الآن';
    if(ms < 3600000) return `قبل ${Math.floor(ms/60000)} دقيقة`;
    if(ms < 86400000) return `قبل ${Math.floor(ms/3600000)} ساعة`;
    return `قبل ${Math.floor(ms/86400000)} يوم`;
  } catch(e){ return null; }
}
function hasRecentBackup(maxAgeMs){
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LAST_BACKUP_KEY);
    if(!raw) return false;
    return (Date.now() - parseInt(raw, 10)) < (maxAgeMs || 86400000);
  } catch(e){ return false; }
}
function downloadJSON(obj, filename){
  const blob = new Blob(['\ufeff', JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2)], {type:'application/json;charset=utf-8'});
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url; a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1500);
}
loadPersistedData();

FILE: /home/administrator/TPA/js/translate-dom.js

// DOM-walking translation: translates all text nodes and attributes
const _i18nOrig = new WeakMap();
function translateDOM(root){
  if(!root) root = document.body;
  if(!root) return;
  const lang = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') ? currentLang : 'ar';
  if(lang === 'ar') return;
  if(!window.TRANSLATE_DICT) return;

  const dict = window.TRANSLATE_DICT;

  // Walk text nodes
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, null, false);
  const nodes = [];
  let node;
  while(node = walker.nextNode()){ nodes.push(node); }

  nodes.forEach(n => {
    if(!_i18nOrig.has(n)) _i18nOrig.set(n, n.nodeValue);
    const original = n.nodeValue;
    if(!original || !original.trim()) return;
    let text = original;
    // Normalize non-breaking spaces for matching
    const normalized = text.replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/&nbsp;/g, ' ').trim();
    // Try exact match first
    if(dict[normalized]){
      n.nodeValue = original.replace(text.trim(), dict[normalized]);
      return;
    }
    const trimmed = text.trim();
    if(dict[trimmed]){
      n.nodeValue = original.replace(trimmed, dict[trimmed]);
      return;
    }
    // Exact match only (partial matching causes mixed-language artifacts)
    // Already handled above
    if(text !== original){
      n.nodeValue = text;
    }
  });

  // Translate placeholders
  root.querySelectorAll('[placeholder]').forEach(el => {
    if(!el.hasAttribute('data-orig-ph')) el.setAttribute('data-orig-ph', el.getAttribute('placeholder'));
    const ph = el.getAttribute('data-orig-ph');
    if(ph && dict[ph]){
      el.setAttribute('placeholder', dict[ph]);
    } else if(ph){
      // Partial match for placeholder
      let t = ph;
      const keys = Object.keys(dict).sort((a,b) => b.length - a.length);
      for(const key of keys){
        if(key.length < 2) continue;
        if(t.includes(key)){ t = t.split(key).join(dict[key]); }
      }
      if(t !== ph) el.setAttribute('placeholder', t);
    }
  });

  // Translate titles
  root.querySelectorAll('[title]').forEach(el => {
    const t = el.getAttribute('title');
    if(t && dict[t]){
      el.setAttribute('title', dict[t]);
    }
  });
}

function restoreDOM(root){
  if(!root) root = document.body;
  if(!root) return;
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, null, false);
  const nodes = [];
  let node;
  while(node = walker.nextNode()){ nodes.push(node); }
  nodes.forEach(n => {
    const orig = _i18nOrig.get(n);
    if(orig !== undefined) n.nodeValue = orig;
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('[placeholder]').forEach(el => {
    const orig = el.getAttribute('data-orig-ph');
    if(orig !== null) el.setAttribute('placeholder', orig);
  });
}
// Auto-translate is triggered by callers (initApp, applyPage, setLang)

// Run translateDOM multiple times to handle nested/partial translations
function translateDOMMulti(){
  if(typeof translateDOM !== 'function') return;
  const lang = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') ? currentLang : 'ar';
  if(lang === 'ar') return;
  translateDOM(document.body);
  setTimeout(() => translateDOM(document.body), 100);
  setTimeout(() => translateDOM(document.body), 500);
  setTimeout(() => translateDOM(document.body), 1500);
}

// Periodic translation to catch re-rendered content
setInterval(function(){
  if(document.hidden) return;
  const lang = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') ? currentLang : 'ar';
  if(lang === 'ar') return;
  if(typeof translateDOM === 'function') translateDOM(document.body);
}, 8000);

// MutationObserver: translate new content as it's added
(function(){
  if(typeof MutationObserver === 'undefined') return;
  const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations){
    const lang = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') ? currentLang : 'ar';
    if(lang === 'ar') return;
    mutations.forEach(function(m){
      m.addedNodes.forEach(function(node){
        if(node.nodeType === 1){ // Element — skip; 8s ticker + initial walk already cover this
          return;
        } else if(node.nodeType === 3){ // Text node
          const text = node.nodeValue.trim();
          if(text && window.TRANSLATE_DICT && window.TRANSLATE_DICT[text]){
            node.nodeValue = node.nodeValue.replace(text, window.TRANSLATE_DICT[text]);
          }
        }
      });
    });
  });
  if(document.body){
    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } else {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
      observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    });
  }
})();

let trashFilter = 'all', trashSearch = '', trashDebounce = null;
function bindTrashControls(){
  $('#trashSearch').addEventListener('input', e => { clearTimeout(trashDebounce); trashDebounce = setTimeout(() => { trashSearch = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); renderTrash(); }, 200); });
  $('#trashFilterChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#trashFilterChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); trashFilter = chip.dataset.trf; renderTrash();
  });
  $('#emptyTrashBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { if(!trash.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','سلة المحذوفات فارغة'); return; } showDelete('إفراغ سلة المحذوفات', `سيتم حذف ${trash.length} عنصر نهائياً. لا يمكن التراجع عن هذا الإجراء.`, () => { trash = []; renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تم إفراغ السلة'); }); });
  $('#trashBulkRestore').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const ids = $$('#trashList .trash-cb:checked').map(cb => cb.dataset.id);
    if(!ids.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','حدد'); return; }
    let r = 0; ids.forEach(id => { if(restoreTrashItem(id)) r++; });
    trash = trash.filter(t => !ids.includes(t.id));
    renderAll(); showSuccess('تم', `${r}`);
  });
  $('#trashBulkDelete').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const ids = $$('#trashList .trash-cb:checked').map(cb => cb.dataset.id);
    if(!ids.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','حدد'); return; }
    showDelete('حذف نهائي', `سيتم حذف ${ids.length} عنصر نهائياً. لا يمكن التراجع عن هذا الإجراء.`, () => { trash = trash.filter(t => !ids.includes(t.id)); renderAll(); showSuccess('تم',`تم حذف ${ids.length} عنصر`); });
  });
  $('#trashSelectAll').addEventListener('change', e => { $$('#trashList .trash-cb').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = e.target.checked; }); $$('#trashList .trash-row').forEach(r => r.classList.toggle('selected', e.target.checked)); updateTrashBulkBar(); });
  $('#trashList').addEventListener('change', e => { if(e.target.classList.contains('trash-cb')){ e.target.closest('.trash-row').classList.toggle('selected', e.target.checked); updateTrashBulkBar(); } });
  $('#trashList').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const res = e.target.closest('[data-restore-id]');
    if(res){ const id = res.dataset.restoreId; if(restoreTrashItem(id)){ trash = trash.filter(t => t.id !== id); renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تمت الاستعادة'); } else showWarning('تعذّر','لا يمكن استعادة هذا العنصر (قد يكون مكرراً)'); return; }
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-permanent-delete-id]');
    if(del){ const id = del.dataset.permanentDeleteId; const item = trash.find(t => t.id === id); if(!item) return; showDelete('حذف نهائي', `سيتم حذف "${item.name}" نهائياً. لا يمكن التراجع عن هذا الإجراء.`, () => { trash = trash.filter(t => t.id !== id); renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تم الحذف'); }); }
  });
}
function updateTrashBulkBar(){ const n = $$('#trashList .trash-cb:checked').length; $('#trashBulkCount').textContent = n; $('#trashBulkBar').classList.toggle('show', n > 0); }
function restoreTrashItem(id){
  const item = trash.find(t => t.id === id); if(!item) return false;
  switch(item.type){
    case 'claim': { const c = {...item.data}; if(typeof recalcClaim==='function') recalcClaim(c); claims.push(c); return true; }
    case 'provider': if(providers.find(p => p.id === item.data.id)) return false; providers.push({...item.data}); return true;
    case 'contract': if(contracts.find(c => c.id === item.data.id)) return false; contracts.push({...item.data}); return true;
    case 'tariff': if(tariffs.find(t => t.id === item.data.id)) return false; tariffs.push({...item.data}); return true;
    case 'batch': if(batches.find(b => b.id === item.data.id)) return false; batches.push({...item.data}); return true;
    case 'service': { const t = tariffs.find(x => x.id === item.data.tariffId); if(!t) return false; if(!t.services) t.services = []; t.services.push({...item.data.service}); return true; }
  }
  return false;
}
function getTrashItemDetails(item){
  switch(item.type){
    case 'provider': return `${item.data.type||''} — ${item.data.city||''}`;
    case 'contract': { const p = getProviderById(item.data.providerId); return `${p?p.name:'—'} — ${item.data.discountRate||0}%`; }
    case 'tariff': return `${TARIFF_TYPES[item.data.type]?.label||''} — ${item.data.services?.length||0}`;
    case 'batch': { const p = getProviderById(item.data.providerId); return `${p?p.name:'—'} — ${formatMonth(item.data.month)}`; }
    case 'claim': return `${item.data.patient} — ${fmtC(item.data.billed||0)}`;
    default: return '';
  }
}
function renderTrash(){
  const total = trash.length;
  const cc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'claim').length;
  const pc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'provider').length;
  const ctc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'contract').length;
  const tc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'tariff').length;
  const bc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'batch').length;
  $('#trashInfo').textContent = `${total} عنصر`;
  const list = trash.filter(item => {
    if(trashFilter !== 'all' && item.type !== trashFilter) return false;
    if(trashSearch){ const q = trashSearch; return item.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || item.typeLabel.toLowerCase().includes(q) || getTrashItemDetails(item).toLowerCase().includes(q); }
    return true;
  }).sort((a,b) => new Date(b.deletedAt) - new Date(a.deletedAt));
  const box = $('#trashList');
  if(!list.length){ box.innerHTML = `<div class="trash-empty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M3 6h18M8 6V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2"/><path d="M19 6v14a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V6"/></svg><p>${trash.length?'لا نتائج':'فارغة'}</p></div>`; updateTrashBulkBar(); return; }
  box.innerHTML = list.map(item => {
    const d = new Date(item.deletedAt).toISOString().slice(0,10);
    return `<div class="trash-row" data-trash-id="${item.id}"><input type="checkbox" class="trash-cb" data-id="${item.id}"><span class="badge trash-${item.type}">${item.typeLabel}</span><div class="trash-info"><div class="trash-name">${escapeHtml(item.name)}</div><div class="trash-details">${escapeHtml(getTrashItemDetails(item))}</div></div><div class="trash-date">${d}</div><div class="trash-actions"><button class="btn-xs restore" data-restore-id="${item.id}">استعادة</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-permanent-delete-id="${item.id}">حذف</button></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  updateTrashBulkBar(); updateTrashBadge();
}

function levenshtein(a, b){
  a = String(a||''); b = String(b||'');
  if(!a.length) return b.length; if(!b.length) return a.length;
  const m = Array.from({length:a.length+1}, () => Array(b.length+1).fill(0));
  for(let i=0;i<=a.length;i++) m[i][0]=i;
  for(let j=0;j<=b.length;j++) m[0][j]=j;
  for(let i=1;i<=a.length;i++) for(let j=1;j<=b.length;j++)
    m[i][j] = a[i-1]===b[j-1] ? m[i-1][j-1] : 1+Math.min(m[i-1][j],m[i][j-1],m[i-1][j-1]);
  return m[a.length][b.length];
}
function levenshteinSimilarity(a, b){
  const ml = Math.max(String(a||'').length, String(b||'').length);
  if(ml === 0) return 100;
  return Math.round((1 - levenshtein(a,b)/ml) * 100);
}

async function hashPassword(pwd){
  try {
    if(window.crypto && crypto.subtle){
      const buf = new TextEncoder().encode(pwd + '::tpa_salt_2025');
      const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', buf);
      return 'sha256_' + Array.from(new Uint8Array(hash)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
    }
  } catch(e){}
  let h = 0;
  for(let i = 0; i < pwd.length; i++){ h = ((h << 5) - h + pwd.charCodeAt(i)) | 0; }
  return 'simple_' + Math.abs(h).toString(16);
}
async function verifyPassword(pwd){
  try {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem(PWD_HASH_KEY);
    if(!stored) return pwd === PASSWORD;
    const hash = await hashPassword(pwd);
    return hash === stored;
  } catch(e){ return false; }
}
async function changePassword(oldPwd, newPwd){
  const ok = await verifyPassword(oldPwd);
  if(!ok) throw new Error('كلمة المرور الحالية غير صحيحة');
  if(!newPwd || newPwd.length < 6) throw new Error('كلمة المرور الجديدة قصيرة (6 أحرف على الأقل)');
  const hash = await hashPassword(newPwd);
  localStorage.setItem(PWD_HASH_KEY, hash);
}
function daysFromNow(n){ const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate()+n); return d.toISOString().slice(0,10); }
function formatDate(d){ return d || '—'; }
function formatMonth(m){
  if(!m) return '—';
  const months = ['يناير','فبراير','مارس','أبريل','مايو','يونيو','يوليو','أغسطس','سبتمبر','أكتوبر','نوفمبر','ديسمبر'];
  const p = m.split('-');
  return months[parseInt(p[1], 10)-1] + ' ' + p[0];
}
function toDate(v){
  if(!v) return null;
  if(v instanceof Date) return isNaN(v.getTime()) ? null : v;
  const d = new Date(v);
  return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
}
function resolveDateKeyword(v){
  if(!v) return null;
  const s = String(v).trim().toLowerCase();
  const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  const fmtD = d => d.toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(s === 'today' || s === 'now') return fmtD(today);
  if(s === 'yesterday') return fmtD(new Date(today.getTime()-86400000));
  if(s === 'tomorrow') return fmtD(new Date(today.getTime()+86400000));
  if(s === 'start_of_month'){ const d=new Date(today); d.setDate(1); return fmtD(d); }
  if(s === 'end_of_month'){ const d=new Date(today); d.setMonth(d.getMonth()+1,0); return fmtD(d); }
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s)) return s.slice(0,10);
  return s;
}
function daysBetween(a, b){
  const da = toDate(a), db = toDate(b);
  if(!da || !db) return null;
  return Math.round((db - da) / 86400000);
}
function daysRemaining(endDate){
  if(!endDate) return null;
  return Math.ceil((new Date(endDate) - new Date()) / (1000*60*60*24));
}
function daysRemainingText(endDate){
  if(!endDate) return '—';
  const d = daysRemaining(endDate);
  if(d < 0) return `منتهي منذ ${Math.abs(d)} يوم`;
  if(d === 0) return 'ينتهي اليوم';
  return `${d} يوم`;
}
function daysRemainingClass(endDate){
  const d = daysRemaining(endDate);
  if(d === null) return '';
  if(d < 0) return 'bad';
  if(d <= 60) return 'warn';
  return 'good';
}
function countWorkingDays(a, b){
  const da = toDate(a), db = toDate(b);
  if(!da || !db) return 0;
  const start = da < db ? da : db, end = da < db ? db : da;
  let count = 0; const cur = new Date(start);
  while(cur <= end){ const dow = cur.getDay(); if(dow !== 5 && dow !== 6) count++; cur.setDate(cur.getDate()+1); }
  return count;
}
function isBusinessDay(d){ if(!d) return false; const dow = d.getDay(); return dow !== 5 && dow !== 6; }
function isSameWeek(a, b){
  if(!a || !b) return false;
  const sow = d => { const x = new Date(d); x.setDate(x.getDate()-x.getDay()); x.setHours(0,0,0,0); return x.getTime(); };
  return sow(a) === sow(b);
}
function dayOfYear(d){ if(!d) return null; const s = new Date(d.getFullYear(),0,0); return Math.floor((d-s)/86400000); }
function getDaysInMonth(d){ return d ? new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth()+1, 0).getDate() : null; }
function getDaysInYear(d){ if(!d) return null; const y = d.getFullYear(); return ((y%4===0 && y%100!==0) || y%400===0) ? 366 : 365; }
function normalizeArabic(str){
  return String(str||'').replace(/[\u064B-\u065F\u0670\u06D6-\u06ED]/g,'').replace(/\u0640/g,'')
    .replace(/[إأآا]/g,'ا').replace(/ى/g,'ي').replace(/ؤ/g,'و').replace(/ئ/g,'ي').replace(/ة/g,'ه')
    .toLowerCase().trim();
}
function toEnglishDigits(s){ return String(s||'').replace(/[٠-٩]/g, d => String.fromCharCode(d.charCodeAt(0) - 0x0660 + 48)); }
function parseNumber(val){
  if(typeof val === 'number' && !isNaN(val)) return val;
  if(val === null || val === undefined || val === '') return 0;
  let s = toEnglishDigits(String(val)).replace(/[,\u066C]/g,'').replace(/\u066B/g,'.').replace(/[^\d.\-+eE]/g,'');
  const n = parseFloat(s);
  return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n;
}
function roundMoney(n){ return Math.round((Number(n)||0) * 100) / 100; }
function roundToNearest(v, step){ const s = parseNumber(step)||1; return Math.round(parseNumber(v)/s)*s; }
function isMultipleOf(v, step){ const s = parseNumber(step)||1; if(s === 0) return false; const m = Math.abs(parseNumber(v) % s); return m < 0.001 || Math.abs(m-s) < 0.001; }
function pctOf(v, base){ const b = parseNumber(base); if(b === 0) return null; return (parseNumber(v)/b)*100; }
function pctDiff(a, b){ const bv = parseNumber(b); if(bv === 0) return null; return Math.abs((parseNumber(a)-bv)/bv*100); }
